<!doctype html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1,viewport-fit=cover"><style>:root{padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}body{margin:0}img{max-width:100%}[hidden]{display:none!important}</style></head><body>
<title>Daily Pages Library</title>
<meta name="claude:viewport-fit-sides" content="cover">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Literata:ital,opsz,wght@0,7..72,400;0,7..72,600;1,7..72,400&family=Nunito+Sans:opsz,wght@6..12,400;6..12,600;6..12,700&display=swap">
<style>
/* Layout: a quiet reading desk. Library shelf of book cards, then one book's daily page with a painted scene, a 15-minute timer and a reading log. */
:root{
  --bg:#eef1ec; --surface:#fbfcf9; --sunk:#e4e9e2; --ink:#22302a; --muted:#5b6a62; --line:#d5ddd3;
  --accent:#3d6b5a; --on-accent:#fbfcf9; --gold:#a8813f; --good:#3e7d57;
  --f-read:"Literata", Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif;
  --f-ui:"Nunito Sans", system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
  --r:14px;
}
@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){
  --bg:#131915; --surface:#1b231e; --sunk:#222c26; --ink:#e3eae5; --muted:#9aa9a0; --line:#2d3a33;
  --accent:#8fc2ab; --on-accent:#10211a; --gold:#d8b878; --good:#7fc79a; color-scheme:dark}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{
  --bg:#131915; --surface:#1b231e; --sunk:#222c26; --ink:#e3eae5; --muted:#9aa9a0; --line:#2d3a33;
  --accent:#8fc2ab; --on-accent:#10211a; --gold:#d8b878; --good:#7fc79a; color-scheme:dark}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
[hidden]{display:none!important}
body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:16px/1.6 var(--f-ui);margin:0}
body::before{content:"";position:fixed;inset:0 0 auto;height:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);z-index:10;background:var(--bg)}
.wrap>*{min-width:0}
.page>*{min-width:0}
.wrap{max-width:980px;margin:0 auto;padding-left:max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-left,0px));padding-right:max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-right,0px));padding-block:22px 64px;display:grid;gap:22px}
h1,h2,h3{font-family:var(--f-read);font-weight:600;margin:0;text-wrap:balance;line-height:1.2}
h1{font-size:clamp(1.7rem,4vw,2.3rem)}
h2{font-size:1.35rem}
h3{font-size:1.05rem}
.eyebrow{font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700}
.muted{color:var(--muted)}
button,input,textarea,select{font:inherit;color:inherit}
:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--accent);outline-offset:2px}
.btn{border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface);border-radius:999px;padding:9px 16px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer}
.btn.primary{background:var(--accent);color:var(--on-accent);border-color:var(--accent)}
.btn.ghost{background:transparent}
.btn:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default}
header.top{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px 18px;align-items:end;justify-content:space-between}
.brand p{margin:4px 0 0;color:var(--muted);max-width:52ch}
.save{font-size:.82rem;color:var(--muted)}
.acct{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:8px 14px;padding:12px 16px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r);background:var(--surface);font-size:.94rem}
.acct .dot{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--good);flex:none}
.acct.out .dot{background:var(--gold)}
.acct .grow{flex:1;min-width:0}
.stats{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:12px}
.stat{background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r);padding:14px 16px}
.stat .v{font:600 1.6rem/1.1 var(--f-read);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.stat .l{font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px}
/* shelf */
.shelf{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(250px,1fr));gap:14px}
.book{--h:var(--accent);text-align:left;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r);padding:0;cursor:pointer;overflow:hidden;display:grid;grid-template-rows:auto 1fr}
.book:hover{border-color:var(--h)}
.book.active{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--h)}
.spine{height:74px;position:relative;background:var(--h)}
.spine canvas{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%}
.book .b{padding:14px 16px 16px;display:grid;gap:6px}
.book .t{font:600 1.05rem/1.25 var(--f-read)}
.book .a{font-size:.86rem;color:var(--muted)}
.bar{height:6px;border-radius:99px;background:var(--sunk);overflow:hidden}
.bar span{display:block;height:100%;background:var(--h);width:0}
.book .meta{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
/* calendar */
.card{background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r);padding:18px}
.cal{display:grid;grid-auto-flow:column;grid-template-rows:repeat(7,14px);gap:4px;overflow-x:auto;padding-bottom:4px}
.cal i{width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--sunk);display:block}
.cal i.l1{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 35%,var(--sunk))}
.cal i.l2{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 65%,var(--sunk))}
.cal i.l3{background:var(--accent)}
.cal i.today{outline:2px solid var(--gold);outline-offset:1px}
.legend{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;font-size:.78rem;color:var(--muted);margin-top:8px}
.legend i{width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;display:inline-block}
/* today page */
.page{--h:var(--accent);display:grid;gap:18px}
.scene{position:relative;border-radius:var(--r);overflow:hidden;aspect-ratio:21/8;max-width:100%;background:var(--h)}
.scene canvas,.scene img{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block}
.scene .cap{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;padding:18px 20px;color:#fdfdfa;background:linear-gradient(transparent,rgba(10,20,16,.55));font:italic 400 clamp(1rem,2.4vw,1.25rem)/1.4 var(--f-read);text-shadow:0 1px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.35)}
.reading{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.35fr) minmax(0,1fr);gap:18px;align-items:start}
.assign{display:grid;gap:12px}
.dayline{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px 14px;align-items:baseline;justify-content:space-between}
.pager{display:flex;gap:6px}
.pager .btn{padding:6px 12px}
.secs{margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none;display:grid;gap:4px}
.secs li{font:400 1.12rem/1.35 var(--f-read)}
.secs li b{font:700 .78rem var(--f-ui);letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--h);margin-right:8px}
.pages{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px 18px;align-items:center;padding:14px 16px;border-radius:12px;background:var(--sunk)}
.pages .big{font:600 1.5rem/1 var(--f-read);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.pages .small{font-size:.82rem;color:var(--muted)}
.prompt{border-left:3px solid var(--h);padding:4px 0 4px 14px;font:italic 400 1.02rem/1.5 var(--f-read);color:var(--ink)}
.file{font-size:.82rem;color:var(--muted);word-break:break-word}
.timer{display:grid;gap:12px;justify-items:center;text-align:center}
.ring{width:180px;height:180px;max-width:100%}
.ring circle{fill:none;stroke-width:9}
.ring .track{stroke:var(--sunk)}
.ring .prog{stroke:var(--h);stroke-linecap:round;transition:stroke-dashoffset .5s linear}
.ring text{font:600 34px var(--f-read);fill:var(--ink);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.ring .sub{font:600 11px var(--f-ui);fill:var(--muted);letter-spacing:.1em}
.row{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center}
.log{display:grid;gap:14px}
.fields{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:12px}
label.f{display:grid;gap:6px;font-size:.84rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted)}
input[type=number],textarea{width:100%;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--bg);padding:10px 12px;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink)}
textarea{min-height:84px;resize:vertical;font-family:var(--f-read);font-size:1rem}
.moods{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.moods button{border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface);border-radius:999px;padding:6px 12px;cursor:pointer;font-size:.9rem}
.moods button[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--h);border-color:var(--h);color:#fff}
.done-row{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between}
.plan{display:grid;gap:4px}
.plan button{display:grid;grid-template-columns:52px minmax(0,1fr) auto;gap:10px;align-items:center;text-align:left;background:transparent;border:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding:10px 4px;cursor:pointer}
.plan button:hover{background:var(--sunk)}
.plan .n{font:600 .82rem var(--f-ui);color:var(--muted);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.plan .tt{font:400 .98rem/1.35 var(--f-read);min-width:0}
.plan .pp{font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted);white-space:nowrap;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.plan button.done .n{color:var(--good)}
.plan button.cur{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--h) 12%,transparent)}
.links{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px}
.links a{color:var(--accent);font-weight:700}
.toast{position:fixed;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);bottom:calc(18px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);padding:10px 18px;border-radius:999px;font-weight:700;font-size:.9rem;z-index:20}
@media (max-width:760px){.reading{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}.stats{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}.scene{aspect-ratio:16/9}}
@media (max-width:440px){.fields{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{transition:none!important}}
</style>

<div class="wrap">
  <header class="top">
    <div class="brand">
      <div class="eyebrow">Fifteen minutes a day</div>
      <h1>Daily Pages Library</h1>
      <p>One short reading from your own copy each day, with a timer, a reading log and a streak to keep the habit alive.</p>
    </div>
    <span class="save" id="save-status">Loading your shelf…</span>
  </header>

  <div class="acct out" id="acct" role="status"><span class="dot"></span><span class="grow" id="acct-text">Checking your sign-in…</span></div>

  <section class="stats" id="stats" aria-label="Reading summary"></section>

  <section aria-labelledby="shelf-h" style="display:grid;gap:12px">
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <h2 id="shelf-h">Your shelf</h2><span class="muted" style="font-size:.88rem">Tap a book to open today's reading</span>
    </div>
    <div class="shelf" id="shelf"></div>
  </section>

  <section class="page" id="page" aria-live="polite"></section>

  <section class="card" aria-labelledby="cal-h">
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:12px">
      <h3 id="cal-h">Reading days, last 20 weeks</h3><span class="muted" style="font-size:.86rem" id="cal-note"></span>
    </div>
    <div class="cal" id="cal"></div>
    <div class="legend"><span>Less</span><i style="background:var(--sunk)"></i><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 35%,var(--sunk))"></i><i style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 65%,var(--sunk))"></i><i style="background:var(--accent)"></i><span>15+ min</span></div>
  </section>
</div>
<div class="toast" id="toast" hidden></div>

(function(){
const LINES=["Small pages, read daily, become a different mind.","Read slowly enough to let one idea stay.","Fifteen quiet minutes is a gift you give your future self.","Today, read to understand, not to finish.","A single sentence can change the shape of a day.","Breathe in. Turn the page. Begin.","The habit matters more than the speed.","Read as if the author were sitting beside you.","Let today's pages become tonight's action.","Calm attention is its own kind of strength.","Every chapter you finish is a promise you kept.","Notice the line you want to remember.","Growth is quiet. It happens one page at a time.","You don't need more time. You need this time.","Read it once for the idea, once for your life.","Let the words settle before you move on.","Progress, not perfection, page by page.","Underline less. Live it more.","What you read today, you carry tomorrow.","A steady reader becomes a steady person.","Choose one idea to practise before sunset.","Rest your phone. Open your book.","The best time to read was yesterday. The next best is now.","Ideas grow when you give them room.","A small daily habit outlasts a big burst of effort.","Be curious about the page in front of you.","Today's reading is a conversation, not a test.","Gentle and consistent wins.","Let this be the calm part of your day.","Notice how you feel after you read.","One page of focus beats ten of hurry.","You are building yourself with every session.","Read, reflect, respond.","Make room for the thought you didn't expect.","The streak is a mirror of your commitment.","Quiet mind, open page.","Learn something today you can teach tomorrow.","This is your fifteen minutes. Protect them.","Return to the line that moved you.","Finish today gently, and begin again tomorrow."];
const PROMPTS=["Before you read: what do you hope these pages will give you today?","While you read: mark the one sentence you'd write on a card.","Before you read: what is on your mind right now? Let it rest for 15 minutes.","While you read: notice one idea you disagree with, and why.","Before you read: which part of your life could use this book today?","While you read: look for one example you can copy this week.","Before you read: take three slow breaths, then begin.","While you read: ask, \"Where have I seen this in my own life?\""];
const MOODS=[["1","Drained"],["2","Low"],["3","Steady"],["4","Bright"],["5","Inspired"]];
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const byId=Object.fromEntries(BOOKS.map(b=>[b.id,b]));
const dark=()=>matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches&&document.documentElement.dataset.theme!=="light"||document.documentElement.dataset.theme==="dark";
const hueOf=b=>dark()?b.hueD:b.hue;
const today=()=>{const d=new Date();return d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")};
let S={v:1,active:BOOKS[0].id,books:{},log:{}},store=null;
const LKEY="daily-pages-v1";
function bk(id){if(!S.books[id])S.books[id]={cur:1,days:{}};return S.books[id]}
function day(id,n){const b=bk(id);if(!b.days[n])b.days[n]={};return b.days[n]}
function normalize(x){if(!x||typeof x!=="object")return S;const o={v:1,active:byId[x.active]?x.active:BOOKS[0].id,books:x.books&&typeof x.books==="object"?x.books:{},log:x.log&&typeof x.log==="object"?x.log:{}};
  BOOKS.forEach(b=>{const s=o.books[b.id];if(s){s.cur=Math.min(b.days.length,Math.max(1,+s.cur||1));if(!s.days||typeof s.days!=="object")s.days={}}});return o}
const status=t=>$("save-status").textContent=t;
function localLoad(){try{const r=localStorage.getItem(LKEY);return r?JSON.parse(r):null}catch(e){return null}}
function localSave(){try{localStorage.setItem(LKEY,JSON.stringify(S));return true}catch(e){return false}}
let tmr=null,busy=false,again=false;
function save(){clearTimeout(tmr);status("Saving…");tmr=setTimeout(flush,600)}
async function flush(){if(busy){again=true;return}busy=true;
  try{if(store&&store.kind==="db"){await store.ref.set(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S)));status("Saved to your account")}else status(localSave()?"Saved in this browser":"Not saved: storage unavailable")}
  catch(e){status(localSave()?"Saved in this browser":"Couldn't save right now")}
  busy=false;if(again){again=false;flush()}}
function toast(t){const el=$("toast");el.textContent=t;el.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.t);toast.t=setTimeout(()=>el.hidden=true,2200)}

/* seeded calm landscape */
function rng(seed){let h=2166136261;for(const c of seed)h=Math.imul(h^c.charCodeAt(0),16777619);return()=>{h+=0x6D2B79F5;let t=h;t=Math.imul(t^t>>>15,t|1);t^=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,t|61);return((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296}}
function mix(a,b,t){const p=x=>[1,3,5].map(i=>parseInt(x.slice(i,i+2),16));const A=p(a),B=p(b);return"rgb("+A.map((v,i)=>Math.round(v+(B[i]-v)*t)).join(",")+")"}
function paint(cv,seed,hue){const r=rng(seed),W=cv.clientWidth||600,H=cv.clientHeight||240,dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);cv.width=W*dpr;cv.height=H*dpr;const g=cv.getContext("2d");g.scale(dpr,dpr);
  const night=dark();const skies=night?[["#1d2a3a","#3a4660"],["#1f2b2e","#47585a"],["#241f36","#4d4466"]]:[["#dfe9ee","#f6efe2"],["#e6ece4","#f4eadf"],["#e9e5f1","#f7efe6"],["#dce8e6","#f3f0e4"]];
  const sk=skies[Math.floor(r()*skies.length)];const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);gr.addColorStop(0,sk[0]);gr.addColorStop(1,sk[1]);g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  const sx=W*(.15+r()*.7),sy=H*(.22+r()*.25),sr=Math.min(W,H)*(.09+r()*.06);
  const halo=g.createRadialGradient(sx,sy,sr*.4,sx,sy,sr*3.2);halo.addColorStop(0,night?"rgba(250,240,210,.35)":"rgba(255,236,196,.75)");halo.addColorStop(1,"rgba(255,236,196,0)");g.fillStyle=halo;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  g.fillStyle=night?"#f1ead2":"#fbe6bd";g.beginPath();g.arc(sx,sy,sr,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
  if(r()<.7){g.fillStyle=night?"rgba(255,255,255,.06)":"rgba(255,255,255,.55)";for(let c=0;c<3;c++){const cx=r()*W,cy=H*(.1+r()*.3),cw=W*(.12+r()*.15);g.beginPath();g.ellipse(cx,cy,cw,cw*.16,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill()}}
  const layers=4+Math.floor(r()*2);const far=night?"#4a5a66":"#c9d6d2";
  for(let L=0;L<layers;L++){const t=L/(layers-1);const col=mix(far,hue,.25+t*.7);const base=H*(.48+t*.4),amp=H*(.05+r()*.09)*(1-t*.3),f1=1+r()*2.5,f2=3+r()*5,ph=r()*6;
    g.fillStyle=col;g.globalAlpha=.55+t*.45;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,H);for(let x=0;x<=W;x+=6){const y=base-Math.sin(x/W*Math.PI*f1+ph)*amp-Math.sin(x/W*Math.PI*f2+ph*2)*amp*.35;g.lineTo(x,y)}g.lineTo(W,H);g.closePath();g.fill()}
  g.globalAlpha=1;
  if(r()<.55){const by=H*(.86+r()*.06);g.fillStyle=night?"rgba(160,190,210,.18)":"rgba(255,255,255,.35)";g.fillRect(0,by,W,H-by)}
  if(r()<.6){g.strokeStyle=night?"rgba(230,230,240,.5)":"rgba(40,50,45,.45)";g.lineWidth=1.4;for(let k=0;k<3;k++){const bx=W*(.2+r()*.6),byy=H*(.15+r()*.25),s=4+r()*4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(bx-s,byy);g.quadraticCurveTo(bx-s/2,byy-s*.6,bx,byy);g.quadraticCurveTo(bx+s/2,byy-s*.6,bx+s,byy);g.stroke()}}
}

function stats(){let mins=0,daysRead=0,pages=0;BOOKS.forEach(b=>{const s=S.books[b.id];if(!s)return;Object.values(s.days).forEach(d=>{if(d.done)daysRead++;mins+=+d.min||0;pages+=+d.pg||0})});
  let streak=0;const d=new Date();if(!(S.log[today()]>0))d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);for(;;){const k=d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");if(S.log[k]>0){streak++;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1)}else break}
  const total=BOOKS.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.days.length,0);
  $("stats").textContent="";[[streak,streak===1?"day streak":"day streak"],[daysRead+"/"+total,"readings done"],[mins,"minutes read"],[pages,"pages logged"]].forEach(([v,l])=>{const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="stat";const a=document.createElement("div");a.className="v";a.textContent=v;const c=document.createElement("div");c.className="l";c.textContent=l;e.append(a,c);$("stats").append(e)})}
function shelf(){const el=$("shelf");el.textContent="";BOOKS.forEach(b=>{const s=bk(b.id),done=Object.values(s.days).filter(d=>d.done).length;
  const btn=document.createElement("button");btn.type="button";btn.className="book"+(S.active===b.id?" active":"");btn.style.setProperty("--h",hueOf(b));btn.setAttribute("aria-label",b.title+", "+done+" of "+b.days.length+" readings done");
  const sp=document.createElement("div");sp.className="spine";const cv=document.createElement("canvas");sp.append(cv);
  const body=document.createElement("div");body.className="b";const t=document.createElement("div");t.className="t";t.textContent=b.title;const a=document.createElement("div");a.className="a";a.textContent=b.author+" · "+b.tag;
  const bar=document.createElement("div");bar.className="bar";const sb=document.createElement("span");sb.style.width=(done/b.days.length*100)+"%";bar.append(sb);
  const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="meta";const m1=document.createElement("span");m1.textContent=done+" of "+b.days.length+" days";const m2=document.createElement("span");m2.textContent=done>=b.days.length?"Finished":"Next: Day "+s.cur;m.append(m1,m2);
  body.append(t,a,bar,m);btn.append(sp,body);btn.addEventListener("click",()=>{S.active=b.id;save();renderAll();$("page").scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth",block:"start"})});el.append(btn);
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>paint(cv,b.id+"-spine",hueOf(b)))})}
function cal(){const el=$("cal");el.textContent="";const end=new Date();const start=new Date();start.setDate(end.getDate()-(7*20-1)-end.getDay());let n=0,active=0;
  for(let d=new Date(start);d<=end;d.setDate(d.getDate()+1)){const k=d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");const v=+S.log[k]||0;const i=document.createElement("i");i.className=(v>=15?"l3":v>=8?"l2":v>0?"l1":"")+(k===today()?" today":"");i.title=k+": "+v+" min";el.append(i);if(v>0)active++;n++}
  $("cal-note").textContent=active+" reading days";}

let timer={left:900,run:false,iv:null,key:''};let SIGNED=null;
function page(){clearInterval(timer.iv);timer.run=false;const b=byId[S.active],s=bk(b.id),n=s.cur,tk=b.id+'-'+n;if(timer.key!==tk){timer.key=tk;timer.left=900}const item=b.days[n-1],d=day(b.id,n),h=hueOf(b);const el=$("page");el.textContent="";el.style.setProperty("--h",h);
  const head=document.createElement("div");head.className="dayline";const hh=document.createElement("div");const ey=document.createElement("div");ey.className="eyebrow";ey.textContent=b.title+" · "+b.author;const h2=document.createElement("h2");h2.textContent="Day "+n+" of "+b.days.length;hh.append(ey,h2);
  const pg=document.createElement("div");pg.className="pager";const pv=document.createElement("button");pv.className="btn";pv.textContent="‹ Previous";pv.disabled=n<=1;pv.onclick=()=>{s.cur--;save();page();shelf()};const nx=document.createElement("button");nx.className="btn";nx.textContent="Next ›";nx.disabled=n>=b.days.length;nx.onclick=()=>{s.cur++;save();page();shelf()};pg.append(pv,nx);head.append(hh,pg);
  const sc=document.createElement("div");sc.className="scene";const cv=document.createElement("canvas");sc.append(cv);const cap=document.createElement("div");cap.className="cap";cap.textContent=LINES[(n*7+b.id.length*3)%LINES.length];
  const img=new Image();img.alt="";img.decoding="async";img.onload=()=>{sc.insertBefore(img,cap)};img.src="https://picsum.photos/seed/dp-"+b.id+"-"+n+"/1200/460";sc.append(cap);
  const rd=document.createElement("div");rd.className="reading";
  const as=document.createElement("div");as.className="card assign";const e2=document.createElement("div");e2.className="eyebrow";e2.textContent="Today's reading · about "+item.min+" min";
  const ul=document.createElement("ul");ul.className="secs";item.s.forEach(([lab,t])=>{const li=document.createElement("li");const bb=document.createElement("b");bb.textContent=lab;li.append(bb,t);ul.append(li)});
  if(item.part[1]>1){const li=document.createElement("li");li.className="muted";li.style.fontSize=".9rem";li.textContent="Part "+item.part[0]+" of "+item.part[1]+" of this chapter";ul.append(li)}
  const pgs=document.createElement("div");pgs.className="pages";const big=document.createElement("div");big.className="big";big.textContent="pp. "+item.p[0]+"–"+item.p[1];const sm=document.createElement("div");sm.className="small";sm.textContent=(item.p[1]-item.p[0]+1)+" pages · numbers are "+b.unit+"s in your copy";pgs.append(big,sm);
  const pr=document.createElement("div");pr.className="prompt";pr.textContent=PROMPTS[(n+b.id.length)%PROMPTS.length];
  const fl=document.createElement("div");fl.className="file";fl.textContent="Your file: F:\\ANURAG\\"+b.file;
  const lk=document.createElement("div");lk.className="links";const a1=document.createElement("a");a1.href=b.practice;a1.textContent="Open the 90-day practice workbook";lk.append(a1);
  as.append(e2,ul,pgs,pr,fl,lk);
  const tm=document.createElement("div");tm.className="card timer";const t3=document.createElement("h3");t3.textContent="Reading timer";
  const NS="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";const svg=document.createElementNS(NS,"svg");svg.setAttribute("viewBox","0 0 180 180");svg.setAttribute("class","ring");svg.setAttribute("role","img");
  const mk=(t,a)=>{const e=document.createElementNS(NS,t);for(const k in a)e.setAttribute(k,a[k]);return e};const C=2*Math.PI*78;
  svg.append(mk("circle",{cx:90,cy:90,r:78,class:"track"}));const prg=mk("circle",{cx:90,cy:90,r:78,class:"prog","stroke-dasharray":C,"stroke-dashoffset":0,transform:"rotate(-90 90 90)"});svg.append(prg);
  const tx=mk("text",{x:90,y:96,"text-anchor":"middle"});const st=mk("text",{x:90,y:120,"text-anchor":"middle",class:"sub"});st.textContent="MINUTES LEFT";svg.append(tx,st);
  const draw=()=>{const m=Math.floor(timer.left/60),sec=timer.left%60;tx.textContent=m+":"+String(sec).padStart(2,"0");prg.setAttribute("stroke-dashoffset",C*(1-timer.left/900))};draw();
  const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="row";const go=document.createElement("button");go.className="btn primary";go.id="t-go";const rs=document.createElement("button");rs.className="btn ghost";rs.textContent="Reset";
  const lab=()=>go.textContent=timer.run?"Pause":timer.left<900?"Resume":"Start 15 minutes";lab();
  go.onclick=()=>{if(timer.run){clearInterval(timer.iv);timer.run=false}else{timer.run=true;timer.iv=setInterval(()=>{timer.left--;draw();if(timer.left<=0){clearInterval(timer.iv);timer.run=false;timer.left=900;const dd=day(b.id,s.cur);dd.min=Math.max(+dd.min||0,15);$("f-min").value=dd.min;logMins();save();toast("15 minutes done. Log your takeaway.");lab();draw()}},1000)}lab()};
  rs.onclick=()=>{clearInterval(timer.iv);timer.run=false;timer.left=900;draw();lab()};r.append(go,rs);
  const tn=document.createElement("p");tn.className="muted";tn.style.margin="0";tn.style.fontSize=".86rem";tn.textContent="Phone face down. When the ring empties, 15 minutes are logged for you.";
  tm.append(t3,svg,r,tn);rd.append(as,tm);
  const lg=document.createElement("div");lg.className="card log";const l3=document.createElement("h3");l3.textContent="Today's reading log";
  const fs=document.createElement("div");fs.className="fields";
  const fld=(id,lbl,val,typ)=>{const L=document.createElement("label");L.className="f";L.htmlFor=id;L.textContent=lbl;const i=document.createElement("input");i.type=typ;i.id=id;i.min="0";i.inputMode="numeric";i.value=val??"";L.append(i);return[L,i]};
  const [L1,iMin]=fld("f-min","Minutes read",d.min,"number");const [L2,iPg]=fld("f-pg","Pages read",d.pg,"number");
  iMin.oninput=()=>{d.min=iMin.value===""?null:+iMin.value;logMins();save();stats()};iPg.oninput=()=>{d.pg=iPg.value===""?null:+iPg.value;save();stats()};fs.append(L1,L2);
  const ta=(id,lbl,key,ph)=>{const L=document.createElement("label");L.className="f";L.htmlFor=id;L.textContent=lbl;const t=document.createElement("textarea");t.id=id;t.placeholder=ph;t.value=d[key]||"";t.oninput=()=>{d[key]=t.value;save()};L.append(t);return L};
  const md=document.createElement("div");md.className="f";const ml=document.createElement("span");ml.className="f";ml.style.display="block";ml.textContent="How do you feel after reading?";const mb=document.createElement("div");mb.className="moods";mb.setAttribute("role","group");mb.setAttribute("aria-label","Mood after reading");
  MOODS.forEach(([v,t])=>{const x=document.createElement("button");x.type="button";x.textContent=t;x.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(d.mood===v));x.onclick=()=>{d.mood=d.mood===v?null:v;[...mb.children].forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(c===x&&d.mood===v)));save()};mb.append(x)});md.append(ml,mb);
  const dr=document.createElement("div");dr.className="done-row";const dn=document.createElement("span");dn.className="muted";dn.textContent=d.done?"Completed on "+(d.date||"")+".":"Mark it done when you finish the pages.";
  const db=document.createElement("button");db.className="btn"+(d.done?"":" primary");db.textContent=d.done?"Completed ✓ (undo)":"Mark today's reading done";
  db.onclick=()=>{d.done=!d.done;if(d.done){d.date=today();if(!(+d.min>0)){d.min=15}logMins();if(s.cur<b.days.length)s.cur++;toast("Reading done. See you tomorrow.")}save();renderAll()};dr.append(dn,db);
  lg.append(l3,fs,ta("f-take","One idea I want to keep","take","Write the sentence or idea that stood out…"),ta("f-act","How I'll use it today","act","One small action, before tonight…"),md,dr);
  const pl=document.createElement("details");pl.className="card";const su=document.createElement("summary");su.style.cursor="pointer";su.style.fontWeight="700";su.textContent="Full reading plan · "+b.days.length+" days";const list=document.createElement("div");list.className="plan";list.style.marginTop="10px";
  b.days.forEach((it,i)=>{const k=i+1,dd=s.days[k]||{};const x=document.createElement("button");x.type="button";x.className=(dd.done?"done":"")+(k===n?" cur":"");const a=document.createElement("span");a.className="n";a.textContent=(dd.done?"✓ ":"")+"Day "+k;const t=document.createElement("span");t.className="tt";t.textContent=it.s.map(z=>z[0]+": "+z[1]).join(" · ")+(it.part[1]>1?" (part "+it.part[0]+")":"");const p=document.createElement("span");p.className="pp";p.textContent="pp. "+it.p[0]+"–"+it.p[1];x.append(a,t,p);x.onclick=()=>{s.cur=k;save();page();shelf();el.scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth"})};list.append(x)});
  pl.append(su,list);
  el.append(head,sc,rd,lg,pl);requestAnimationFrame(()=>paint(cv,b.id+"-day-"+n,h))}
function logMins(){const t=today();
  const b=byId[S.active],d=day(b.id,bk(b.id).cur);if(!d.date)d.date=t;S.log=S.log||{};let sum=0;BOOKS.forEach(bb=>{const s=S.books[bb.id];if(s)Object.values(s.days).forEach(x=>{if(x.date===t)sum+=+x.min||0})});S.log[t]=sum;cal();stats()}
function renderAll(){stats();shelf();page();cal();if(SIGNED)acct(SIGNED[0],SIGNED[1])}
function acct(signed,nm){SIGNED=[signed,nm];const el=$("acct"),t=$("acct-text");el.className="acct"+(signed?"":" out");t.textContent="";
  if(signed){const b=document.createElement("b");b.textContent=nm||"you";const ab=byId[S.active],s=bk(ab.id);t.append("Signed in as ",b,". Your shelf saves to your account and opens where you left off: "+ab.title+", Day "+s.cur+".")}
  else t.textContent=window.claude?"You're not signed in, so your reading log is saved only in this browser. Sign in to claude.ai and reopen this page to keep it on every device.":"Your reading log is saved in this browser on this device. Open this page in the same browser to continue where you left off."}
const local=localLoad();if(local)S=normalize(local);renderAll();
matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").addEventListener("change",()=>{shelf();page()});
let rt;addEventListener("resize",()=>{clearTimeout(rt);rt=setTimeout(()=>{shelf();page()},250)});
(async function(){let db=null,user=null;try{if(window.claude&&window.claude.use)[db,user]=await Promise.all([window.claude.use("db"),window.claude.use("user")])}catch(e){}
  let id=null,nm="";try{id=user?await user.id():null}catch(e){}try{if(user&&id){const me=await user.me();nm=(me&&me.name)||""}}catch(e){}
  if(db&&id){try{const ref=db.doc("data/users/"+id+"/library");const snap=await ref.get();store={kind:"db",ref};
      if(snap.exists){S=normalize(snap.data());renderAll();status("Saved to your account")}else if(local){await ref.set(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S)));status("Saved to your account")}else status("Progress saves to your account");
      acct(true,nm);return}catch(e){}}
  store={kind:"local"};acct(false,"");status(local?"Saved in this browser":"Progress saves in this browser")})();
})();
</script>

<style>
.wbov{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:30;background:var(--bg);display:grid;grid-template-rows:auto 1fr}
.wbbar{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)) max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-right,0px)) 10px max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-left,0px));border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface)}
.wbbar b{font-family:var(--f-read);min-width:0}
.wbov iframe{border:0;width:100%;height:100%;background:var(--bg)}
.wbgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:10px}
.wbgrid a{display:block;padding:14px 16px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:var(--surface);color:var(--ink);text-decoration:none;font-weight:700}
.wbgrid a span{display:block;font-weight:400;font-size:.85rem;color:var(--muted)}
</style>

It's the universal reset.\", \"steps\": [\"Quiet place, 15 minutes, eyes closed.\", \"Follow your breath.\", \"Let thoughts pass.\"]}, {\"n\": 7, \"name\": \"Evaluating Dreams\", \"meta\": \"mood 1–22 · 5 min\", \"page\": 193, \"use\": \"Use when: You remember a dream and want to understand it.\", \"steps\": [\"On waking, note how it felt.\", \"Good-feeling dreams mean you're on track.\", \"If it felt bad, find the daytime worry behind it and pivot.\"]}, {\"n\": 8, \"name\": \"Book of Positive Aspects\", \"meta\": \"mood 1–10 · 15 min\", \"page\": 199, \"use\": \"Use when: A person, your job or yourself feels uncomfortable.\", \"steps\": [\"One subject per page.\", \"Ask: \\\"What do I like about this?\\\"\", \"Write 10+ points; reread when needed.\"]}, {\"n\": 9, \"name\": \"Scripting\", \"meta\": \"mood 2–6 · 20 min\", \"page\": 207, \"use\": \"Use when: You feel good and want specific details in a wish.\", \"steps\": [\"Choose one wish.\", \"Write a present-tense scene where it's true.\", \"Read it before sleep.\"]}, {\"n\": 10, \"name\": \"Place Mat\", \"meta\": \"mood 2–11 · 10 min\", \"page\": 211, \"use\": \"Use when: Your to-do list feels too long.\", \"steps\": [\"Line down a page.\", \"Left: I will do today. Right: Life will handle.\", \"Do the left; release the right.\"]}, {\"n\": 11, \"name\": \"Segment Intending\", \"meta\": \"mood 4–11 · 1 min each\", \"page\": 217, \"use\": \"Use when: You want a part of your day to go well.\", \"steps\": [\"List today's segments.\", \"Pause before each.\", \"Say what you want from it.\"]}, {\"n\": 12, \"name\": \"Wouldn't It Be Nice If…?\", \"meta\": \"mood 4–16 · 5 min\", \"page\": 225, \"use\": \"Use when: You're slipping into complaints, or a talk turns negative.\", \"steps\": [\"Start sentences with \\\"Wouldn't it be nice if…\\\"\", \"Keep wishes light.\", \"Say 5–10.\"]}, {\"n\": 13, \"name\": \"Which Thought Feels Better?\", \"meta\": \"mood 4–17 · 10 min\", \"page\": 231, \"use\": \"Use when: You're unsure how you feel, or facing a decision.\", \"steps\": [\"Write a statement.\", \"Write another: better, worse or same?\", \"Build on the better ones until relief.\"]}, {\"n\": 14, \"name\": \"Clearing Clutter for Clarity\", \"meta\": \"mood 4–17 · 20+ min\", \"page\": 239, \"use\": \"Use when: Disorganisation stresses you, or you avoid home.\", \"steps\": [\"Numbered boxes in the room.\", \"Box what's not important right now.\", \"Record which box each item went in.\"]}, {\"n\": 15, \"name\": \"Wallet Process\", \"meta\": \"mood 6–16 · All day\", \"page\": 245, \"use\": \"Use when: You feel short of money.\", \"steps\": [\"Keep an unspent note in your wallet.\", \"Mentally spend it on things you see.\", \"Count how many times you could spend it.\"]}, {\"n\": 16, \"name\": \"Pivoting\", \"meta\": \"mood 8–17 · 1 min\", \"page\": 249, \"use\": \"Use when: You notice you've just said what you don't want.\", \"steps\": [\"Notice the bad feeling.\", \"Ask: \\\"What do I want?\\\"\", \"Hold the new thought 17 seconds.\"]}, {\"n\": 17, \"name\": \"Focus Wheel\", \"meta\": \"mood 8–17 · 20 min\", \"page\": 257, \"use\": \"Use when: Something important feels negative.\", \"steps\": [\"Circle with 12 positions; wish in the centre.\", \"Fill positions with things you already believe.\", \"Re-read the centre; it should feel truer.\"]}, {\"n\": 18, \"name\": \"Finding the Feeling-Place\", \"meta\": \"mood 9–17 · 10 min\", \"page\": 267, \"use\": \"Use when: You want more money, a better job, a happier relationship or a better-feeling body.\", \"steps\": [\"Choose the wish.\", \"Imagine one small moment of having it.\", \"Hold that feeling a few minutes.\"]}, {\"n\": 19, \"name\": \"Releasing Debt\", \"meta\": \"mood 10–22 · 20 min\", \"page\": 271, \"use\": \"Use when: Debt feels heavy.\", \"steps\": [\"One column per payment, biggest to smallest.\", \"Write payment and total owed.\", \"Pay double on the smallest; celebrate each one closed.\"]}, {\"n\": 20, \"name\": \"Turning It Over to the Manager\", \"meta\": \"mood 10–17 · 10 min\", \"page\": 279, \"use\": \"Use when: You have too much to do.\", \"steps\": [\"Imagine a perfect manager who handles what you ask.\", \"The book says that manager is life itself.\", \"List what you hand over; act when inspired.\"]}, {\"n\": 21, \"name\": \"Reclaiming Natural Health\", \"meta\": \"mood 10–22 · 15 min\", \"page\": 283, \"use\": \"Use when: You feel unwell, worried by a diagnosis, or in pain.\", \"steps\": [\"Lie down 15 minutes.\", \"Read slowly: \\\"It's natural for my body to be well.\\\"\", \"Keep following medical advice.\"]}, {\"n\": 22, \"name\": \"Moving Up the Emotional Scale\", \"meta\": \"mood 17–22 · 15 min\", \"page\": 293, \"use\": \"Use when: You feel very bad, after a loss or crisis.\", \"steps\": [\"Name your feeling and number.\", \"Find a thought just slightly better, even anger.\", \"Repeat; any relief is success.\"]}], \"rescueIntro\": \"For hard days. Choose what you feel or what's happening, then try the first process.\", \"callout\": \"If you feel hopeless, can't function for more than two weeks, or have thoughts of harming yourself, please contact a doctor or counsellor. In India, the Tele-MANAS mental health helpline is free and open 24/7: 14416.\", \"rescue\": [[\"Bored, flat (8)\", [16, 4], \"Do one small thing you enjoy.\"], [\"Pessimistic (9)\", [13, 18], \"Talk to an upbeat friend.\"], [\"Frustrated, impatient (10)\", [17, 12], \"Take a 10-minute walk.\"], [\"Overwhelmed (11)\", [10, 20], \"Pick one task; delegate or drop one.\"], [\"Disappointed or doubtful (12–13)\", [13, 8], \"Write 3 things that did work.\"], [\"Worried (14)\", [17, 6], \"Write the worry and one action you can take.\"], [\"Blaming or discouraged (15–16)\", [22, 12], \"Rest; tomorrow is a fresh start.\"], [\"Angry (17)\", [22, 16], \"Move your body; delay important replies.\"], [\"Revenge, hatred, rage (18–19)\", [22, 6], \"Step away from the person and situation.\"], [\"Jealous (20)\", [22, 8], \"Write what their success shows you want.\"], [\"Insecure, guilty, unworthy (21)\", [22, 8], \"Talk to someone who loves you.\"], [\"Fear, grief, despair (22)\", [6, 22], \"Reach out to someone you trust today.\"], [\"Situation: worried about money\", [15, 17], \"List income and expenses; take one saving step.\"], [\"Situation: debt feels heavy\", [19, 17], \"Call the lender about options; pay a little extra on the smallest debt.\"], [\"Situation: health worry\", [21, 6], \"See your doctor; follow treatment.\"], [\"Situation: fight with family or partner\", [8, 11], \"Pause before replying; talk once calm.\"], [\"Situation: stressful workday\", [10, 11], \"Prioritise 3 tasks; take a real break.\"], [\"Situation: can't sleep\", [6, 4], \"No screens 30 minutes before bed.\"], [\"Situation: loss or heartbreak\", [22, 6], \"Let yourself grieve; stay connected to people.\"]], \"days\": [{\"t\": \"Where Am I Now?\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"You can't steer without knowing your starting point. Today's ratings become your \\\"before\\\" picture.\", \"s\": [\"Read the 7 key ideas and the Emotional Scale in the workbook.\", \"Open the Progress tab and rate each life area 1–22 in the Day 1 column.\", \"Note the area that feels worst. That's where the biggest gains are waiting.\"], \"e\": \"Money: 14 (Worry). Work: 10 (Frustration). Family: 3 (Happiness).\", \"a\": \"Set 3 daily phone reminders for your daytime pauses.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rampage of Appreciation\", \"x\": \"#1\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Appreciation is the best-feeling emotion on the scale. Practising it on purpose is the fastest way to lift your mood.\", \"s\": [\"Pick something nearby that pleases you, however small.\", \"Think about why you like it for about 17 seconds, until the feeling grows.\", \"Let it lead to the next thing you appreciate. Keep going for 10 minutes, writing as you go.\"], \"e\": \"I like this chai → Mum taught me to make it → she still calls every Sunday → I'm grateful I can hear her voice…\", \"a\": \"Say a sincere, specific thank-you to one person today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Two Sides of Every Subject\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"You can't always change the topic, but you can choose its better side.\", \"s\": [\"Write 3 complaints you make often.\", \"Next to each, write its wanted side: what you'd like instead.\", \"Read the wanted sides aloud. Notice which feels most believable.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"My house is always messy\\\" → \\\"I want a home that feels calm when I walk in.\\\"\", \"a\": \"When a complaint comes up today, say its wanted side out loud instead.\"}, {\"t\": \"Pivoting All Day\", \"x\": \"#16\", \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Every time you catch yourself on the unwanted side, you get a chance to turn.\", \"s\": [\"Keep a note on your phone.\", \"Each time you notice a complaint, ask: \\\"What do I want?\\\"\", \"Tally every pivot. In the evening, write your best 3.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"My colleague is so slow\\\" → \\\"I want this done smoothly. I'll offer to split the work.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Aim for at least 10 pivots today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Feed a Good Thought for 68 Seconds\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"A thought held 17 seconds pulls in similar thoughts; at 68 seconds it gains real strength.\", \"s\": [\"Pick one pleasant subject: a person, a place, a goal.\", \"Set a 68-second timer and think only good thoughts about it.\", \"Do 3 rounds with 3 subjects. Note which was easiest.\"], \"e\": \"My next holiday: the beach… the sound of waves… fresh fish with family… nowhere to rush…\", \"a\": \"Take a 10-minute walk and do one round while walking.\"}, {\"t\": \"15 Minutes of Quiet\", \"x\": \"#6\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Quieting your mind stops negative thinking too, and lets your natural good feeling return.\", \"s\": [\"Sit somewhere quiet, set a 15-minute timer, close your eyes.\", \"Breathe slowly and follow only your breath.\", \"When a thought comes, notice it, let it go, return to the breath.\"], \"e\": \"A gentle sound like a fan or a ticking clock helps if your breath is hard to follow.\", \"a\": \"Choose a fixed time for tomorrow's quiet minutes.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 1 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Looking back shows you what's working.\", \"s\": [\"Check your mood scores on the Progress tab.\", \"Find your best day: what had you been focusing on?\", \"Do a 5-minute Rampage of Appreciation about this week.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Tell one person one thing you learned this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Which Thought Feels Better?\", \"x\": \"#13\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Your feelings judge thoughts faster than logic. Keep the ones that bring relief.\", \"s\": [\"Write one sentence about a subject that bothers you.\", \"Write another and ask: better, worse, or the same?\", \"Keep the better ones and build on them until you feel relief.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'll never get promoted\\\" → \\\"Promotions take time here\\\" (a bit better) → \\\"I've learned a lot this year\\\" (better) → \\\"I could ask what the next role needs\\\" (relief!).\", \"a\": \"Act on your best \\\"better\\\" thought today, even in a tiny way.\"}, {\"t\": \"Positive Aspects: A Person\", \"x\": \"#8\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"When you focus on what you like about someone, you feel better around them.\", \"s\": [\"Start a notebook: \\\"My Book of Positive Aspects.\\\"\", \"Write the name of someone you find difficult at the top of a page.\", \"List 10 things you like about them. Small things count.\"], \"e\": \"My neighbour keeps his garden neat, is punctual, helped when my car broke down…\", \"a\": \"Say one of those positives to the person, or about them.\"}, {\"t\": \"Positive Aspects: Yourself\", \"x\": \"#8\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"How you feel about yourself is the subject you carry everywhere.\", \"s\": [\"On a new page, write \\\"Me.\\\"\", \"List 15 things you like about yourself: qualities, skills, growth.\", \"Add 5 things you like about your body.\"], \"e\": \"I'm a loyal friend. I learn fast. I didn't give up last year…\", \"a\": \"Do one kind thing for yourself today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Segment Intending\", \"x\": \"#11\", \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"When you decide how each part of your day should go before it starts, it goes better.\", \"s\": [\"In the morning, list today's segments.\", \"At the start of each, pause 10 seconds: \\\"In this part, I want ___.\\\"\", \"In the evening, note which went as intended.\"], \"e\": \"Driving: \\\"I want to arrive safely and relaxed, and enjoy the music.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use it before your most important conversation today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Wouldn't It Be Nice If…?\", \"x\": \"#12\", \"m\": \"10 min\", \"w\": \"A soft way to want something without pressure. It turns complaints into wishes.\", \"s\": [\"Write 10 sentences starting \\\"Wouldn't it be nice if…\\\"\", \"Keep them light and playful, not \\\"must.\\\"\", \"Underline the 3 that feel best.\"], \"e\": \"Wouldn't it be nice if this weekend were relaxed? …if my son enjoyed his new school?\", \"a\": \"When a talk turns negative, say \\\"Wouldn't it be nice if…\\\" and steer it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Clearing Clutter for Clarity\", \"x\": \"#14\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Everything around you affects how you feel. Clutter quietly drains your focus.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one small area: a drawer, a desk, a shelf.\", \"For each item ask: \\\"Is this important to my life right now?\\\"\", \"No → into a numbered box. Note which box, so nothing is lost.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Finish one area completely. Pick the next one for next week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 2 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Compare and keep what works.\", \"s\": [\"Compare your Week 1 and Week 2 mood on the Progress tab.\", \"Which exercise gave you the most relief?\", \"Add it to your personal toolkit list.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Plan one enjoyable activity this weekend, just for fun.\"}, {\"t\": \"Creative Workshop: Body & Home\", \"x\": \"#3\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Thinking about why you want something makes the wish feel lighter. Thinking about when or how adds worry.\", \"s\": [\"Two sheets: \\\"My Body\\\" and \\\"My Home.\\\"\", \"Write 3–5 wishes on each.\", \"Under each, write 2–3 reasons starting \\\"…because.\\\" Skip when, how and who.\"], \"e\": \"I want to feel strong and fit… because I love having energy for my kids.\", \"a\": \"Do one small thing for one wish today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Creative Workshop: Relationships & Work\", \"x\": \"#3\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Four life areas, clearly named, focus your energy.\", \"s\": [\"Two more sheets: \\\"My Relationships\\\" and \\\"My Work.\\\"\", \"Same method: wishes with \\\"…because.\\\"\", \"Star the one wish that excites you most.\"], \"e\": \"I want a team that respects my ideas… because I do my best work when I feel heard.\", \"a\": \"Keep the four sheets where you'll see them.\"}, {\"t\": \"Scripting\", \"x\": \"#9\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Writing as if your wish is already true lets you feel it now.\", \"s\": [\"Take your starred wish.\", \"Write half a page about a day when it's true, in present tense.\", \"Add details: what you see, hear and feel.\"], \"e\": \"It's Monday. My manager asks for my view on the new plan. The team builds on my idea. I feel calm and valued…\", \"a\": \"Read your script once before sleeping.\"}, {\"t\": \"Virtual Reality\", \"x\": \"#4\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Your mind responds to a vivid imagined scene almost like a real one.\", \"s\": [\"Close your eyes and choose a pleasant scene.\", \"Add details: weather, clothes, sounds, smells.\", \"Stay in it 5–10 minutes, just enjoying.\"], \"e\": \"A family dinner on a terrace, warm evening, everyone laughing, nobody in a hurry.\", \"a\": \"Recreate one small detail of the scene in real life.\"}, {\"t\": \"Magical Creation Box\", \"x\": \"#2\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Collecting pictures of what you want keeps your attention on the wanted side.\", \"s\": [\"Find a pretty box, or make a phone folder.\", \"Write on it: \\\"Whatever is in this box, IS.\\\"\", \"Add pictures and notes of what you want; with each say, \\\"I'm glad this is on its way.\\\"\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Add 5 items today and one more each day this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Focus Wheel\", \"x\": \"#17\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"When a wish feels far away, thoughts you already believe build the bridge.\", \"s\": [\"Draw a circle with 12 clock positions; write your wish in the centre.\", \"At each position, write something you already believe that feels a bit good.\", \"Cross out any that feel false. By 12, the centre should feel truer.\"], \"e\": \"Centre: \\\"I feel at ease with money.\\\" → \\\"I've always paid for food.\\\" · \\\"I'm learning to track spending.\\\" · \\\"Money comes to me every month.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Write your strongest statement on a note for tomorrow.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 3 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Notice what has already started to move.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your four Creative Workshop sheets.\", \"Write down any good surprises this week, however minor.\", \"Check your mood trend on the Progress tab.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Celebrate three weeks: do something you enjoy.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money: The Wallet Process\", \"x\": \"#15\", \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Feeling \\\"I have money\\\" instead of \\\"not enough\\\" changes your money mood and choices.\", \"s\": [\"Keep a note you won't spend (e.g. ₹500 or ₹2,000) in your wallet.\", \"All day, mentally spend it on things you see: \\\"I could buy that.\\\"\", \"Count your mental spends tonight.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Write your monthly income and 5 biggest monthly expenses.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money: The Prosperity Game\", \"x\": \"#5\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"This game stretches your imagination about abundance, with no fear of overspending.\", \"s\": [\"Use a notebook as a pretend bank account.\", \"Game day 1: deposit ₹1,000 and spend it on paper, noting each purchase.\", \"Each day, deposit ₹1,000 more than the day before and spend it.\"], \"e\": \"₹1,000: running shoes ₹800, flowers for Mum ₹200.\", \"a\": \"Move a small amount (even ₹100) into savings.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money: Releasing Debt\", \"x\": \"#19\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Looking clearly at your debts, with a plan, turns dread into pride.\", \"s\": [\"One column per monthly payment, biggest to smallest.\", \"Write each payment and the total still owed.\", \"Pay double on the smallest when you can; celebrate each column closed.\"], \"e\": \"Header: \\\"I intend to keep every promise on these payments, and on some I'll do even more.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Check one debt's interest rate, or set a savings goal if you have none.\"}, {\"t\": \"Health: Natural Well-Being\", \"x\": \"#21\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Calm, hopeful thoughts help you feel better and care for yourself better.\", \"s\": [\"Lie down 15 minutes. Read slowly: \\\"It's natural for my body to be well. My body knows how to heal.\\\"\", \"Remember a time you felt fit and well; feel it again for 2 minutes.\", \"Write 3 things your body did for you today.\"], \"e\": \"This supports medical care; it never replaces it.\", \"a\": \"One health step: a 20-minute walk, more water, or booking a check-up.\"}, {\"t\": \"Relationships\", \"x\": \"#8\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"You can't control others, but you control what you notice in them.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one important relationship.\", \"Write 10 positive aspects of that person.\", \"Before your next talk, set an intention: \\\"In this conversation I want ___.\\\"\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Spend 15 minutes of full attention with them: no phone.\"}, {\"t\": \"Work & Time: The Place Mat\", \"x\": \"#10\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Overwhelm comes from feeling you must do everything yourself.\", \"s\": [\"Draw a line down a page.\", \"Left: \\\"I will do today.\\\" Right: \\\"Life will handle.\\\"\", \"Do the left list. Release the right one, and notice what eases.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Ask for help with, or delegate, one task today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Hard Emotions: Climbing the Scale\", \"x\": \"#22\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"On bad days you need a tool that meets you where you are.\", \"s\": [\"Think of a mild old hurt or worry.\", \"Name the emotion and its number.\", \"Write a thought just one step better. Repeat until you feel relief.\"], \"e\": \"Powerless (22) → Angry (17): \\\"It's not fair.\\\" → Frustrated (10): \\\"I'm tired of this.\\\" → Hopeful (6): \\\"Maybe there's another way.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Fill in your personal rescue card in the workbook.\"}, {\"t\": \"Where Am I Now? (Again)\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Measure how far you've come.\", \"s\": [\"Open the Progress tab and fill the Day 30 column for each life area.\", \"Look at your mood chart.\", \"Write the 3 biggest changes you notice in yourself.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Share your progress with someone who supports you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 1 Review: Your Starter Toolkit\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Keep what works, and get ready to go deeper.\", \"s\": [\"Choose your 5 favourite exercises and write them on a card.\", \"Decide which parts of the daily routine you'll keep.\", \"Pick the one life area you most want to change in Phase 2, and do a 10-minute Rampage about it.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate. You finished Phase 1! Tomorrow, Phase 2 begins.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 2 Check-in\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"A fresh starting line for Phase 2 shows you exactly where to aim.\", \"s\": [\"Rate your mood today on the Emotional Scale (1–22).\", \"Look at your Day 30 life-area ratings. Circle the lowest two.\", \"Write one sentence for each: \\\"By Day 60, I'd love this area to feel ___.\\\"\"], \"e\": \"Money is at 14 (Worry). By Day 60 I'd love it to feel like 6 (Hopefulness).\", \"a\": \"Put your two sentences where you'll see them every morning.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rampage on a Hard Subject\", \"x\": \"#1\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Appreciation is easy about things you love. Doing it on a difficult subject loosens its grip.\", \"s\": [\"Pick one of your two circled areas.\", \"Write anything good about it, however small, for 10 minutes. Let each thought lead to the next.\", \"Stop when you notice even a little relief.\"], \"e\": \"Money: \\\"I paid rent this month. I have a skill people pay for. I'm learning to track spending. I like how a full fridge feels…\\\"\", \"a\": \"Say one of those good thoughts out loud to someone today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Positive Aspects of Your Work\", \"x\": \"#8\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Most of your waking hours go to work or daily duties. Liking more of it lifts your whole day.\", \"s\": [\"Head a page \\\"My Work\\\" (job, business, studies or home duties).\", \"List 15 positive aspects: people, skills, comforts, what you've learned.\", \"Star the 3 you'd most miss if they were gone.\"], \"e\": \"My commute has good music time. My colleague Rina is funny. I've become faster at reports…\", \"a\": \"Thank one person at work for something specific.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Pivot Log\", \"x\": \"#16\", \"m\": \"5 min, 3 times\", \"w\": \"Every complaint holds a hidden wish. Turning it around in the moment is the core skill of this book.\", \"s\": [\"Carry a small note or phone note titled \\\"Pivots.\\\"\", \"Each time you notice a complaint, ask: \\\"So what do I want instead?\\\"\", \"Write the complaint → the wish. Aim for 5 today.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"Traffic is awful\\\" → \\\"I want an easy, relaxed drive.\\\" · \\\"He never listens\\\" → \\\"I want to feel heard.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Tonight, read your wishes, not your complaints.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Full Day of Segment Intending\", \"x\": \"#11\", \"m\": \"5 min + all day\", \"w\": \"On Day 11 you tried it once. Doing it for every part of one day shows its full power.\", \"s\": [\"In the morning, list every segment: waking, travel, meetings, meals, calls, evening.\", \"Before each one, pause 10 seconds: \\\"In this part, I want ___.\\\"\", \"Tonight, tick each segment that went as intended.\"], \"e\": \"Lunch: \\\"I want to eat slowly and enjoy it.\\\" Team call: \\\"I want to be clear and kind.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Count your ticks. Write the number: ___ out of ___.\"}, {\"t\": \"68 Seconds on 5 Subjects\", \"x\": \"#4\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Holding a pleasant thought for about 68 seconds gives it real strength. Five subjects spread the good feeling across your life.\", \"s\": [\"Choose 5 subjects: body, home, money, a person, work.\", \"For each, imagine it going well. Hold the picture for about 68 seconds (a slow count to 68).\", \"Note which subject felt easiest and which felt hardest.\"], \"e\": \"Home: sunlight in a tidy living room, tea on the table, the family relaxed.\", \"a\": \"Repeat the hardest one once more before sleep.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 5 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Notice how often you now reach for a tool without being told.\", \"s\": [\"Compare this week's moods with Week 4 on the Progress tab.\", \"Count your pivots and segment ticks from this week.\", \"Write one sentence: \\\"The tool I used most on my own was ___.\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Do something relaxing just for you this weekend.\"}, {\"t\": \"Which Thought Feels Better? on a Long Worry\", \"x\": \"#13\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Old worries feel solid, but they're only thoughts you've practised. Small steps loosen them.\", \"s\": [\"Name a worry you've carried for months or years.\", \"Write a first sentence about it, exactly as it feels now.\", \"Keep writing new sentences. Keep only those that feel a little better. Stop at relief.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"My father and I will never get along\\\" → \\\"We've had a few good talks\\\" → \\\"He did call on my birthday\\\" → \\\"Maybe small, easy talks are enough for now.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take one small step suggested by your last thought.\"}, {\"t\": \"Focus Wheel on Money\", \"x\": \"#17\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Money is where most people feel the biggest gap between wish and belief.\", \"s\": [\"Centre: one money sentence you'd love to believe (e.g. \\\"Money comes to me easily\\\").\", \"Fill all 12 positions with money thoughts you already believe.\", \"Read the centre again. Rate it: believable 0–10.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I've always found a way to pay bills.\\\" · \\\"My income has grown since my first job.\\\" · \\\"I know people who started with less.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do one money task you've been putting off: check a statement, cancel a subscription, or pay a small bill.\"}, {\"t\": \"Focus Wheel on a Relationship\", \"x\": \"#17\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"You can't change another person, but you can change how you feel about them, and that changes how you act.\", \"s\": [\"Centre: how you'd love to feel with this person (e.g. \\\"I feel easy around my sister\\\").\", \"Fill 12 positions with true, gentle thoughts about her and the relationship.\", \"Rate the centre's believability 0–10.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"We laughed at Diwali.\\\" · \\\"She helped me move house.\\\" · \\\"We both love our mother.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Send her a short, warm message with no agenda.\"}, {\"t\": \"Moving Up the Scale on an Old Hurt\", \"x\": \"#22\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"On Day 28 you climbed the scale on a mild hurt. Now try a deeper one, slowly.\", \"s\": [\"Choose an old hurt that still stings. (If it feels too heavy, choose a lighter one or talk to someone you trust.)\", \"Name the feeling and its number.\", \"Write one thought per step up the scale. It's fine to stop at anger or frustration: that's still progress.\"], \"e\": \"Grief (22) → Anger (17): \\\"It wasn't fair.\\\" → Disappointment (12): \\\"I wanted it to go differently.\\\" → Contentment (7): \\\"I've grown since then.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Be extra kind to yourself this evening.\"}, {\"t\": \"A \\\"Wouldn't It Be Nice\\\" Day\", \"x\": \"#12\", \"m\": \"5 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Using the phrase all day turns everyday complaints into light, easy wishes.\", \"s\": [\"Write 5 \\\"Wouldn't it be nice if…\\\" lines in the morning.\", \"During the day, each time you'd normally complain, say one silently instead.\", \"Tonight, write the best 3.\"], \"e\": \"Instead of \\\"This queue is so slow\\\" → \\\"Wouldn't it be nice if the next counter opened?\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use the phrase once out loud to lift a conversation.\"}, {\"t\": \"Meditation Twice a Day\", \"x\": \"#6\", \"m\": \"15 min × 2\", \"w\": \"A quiet mind stops feeding old thoughts. Twice a day makes the calm last longer.\", \"s\": [\"Morning: sit comfortably, eyes closed, 15 minutes. Focus on your breath or a soft sound like a fan.\", \"When a thought comes, notice it and return to the breath.\", \"Evening: repeat. Note which session felt calmer.\"], \"a\": \"Before one tense moment today, take three slow breaths.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 6 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"See which hard subjects have started to feel lighter.\", \"s\": [\"Re-rate the two areas you circled on Day 31.\", \"Which Focus Wheel score rose the most?\", \"Write: \\\"A subject that feels lighter now is ___.\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Tell one supportive person about a small win.\"}, {\"t\": \"Update Your Creative Workshop\", \"x\": \"#3\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Wishes grow as you grow. Updating them keeps your energy fresh.\", \"s\": [\"Reread the four sheets from Days 15–16.\", \"Tick anything that has already happened or moved closer.\", \"Add 2 new wishes to each sheet, each with \\\"…because.\\\"\"], \"e\": \"Ticked: \\\"I want calmer mornings\\\" (I now wake 20 minutes earlier). New: \\\"I want to learn swimming… because I love feeling free in water.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take one small step for one of the new wishes.\"}, {\"t\": \"Script One Week Ahead\", \"x\": \"#9\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Writing your coming week as if it already went well sets a calm, positive tone for it.\", \"s\": [\"Head a page with next week's dates.\", \"Write each day in past tense, as if it went beautifully: \\\"Monday was easy. The meeting went well…\\\"\", \"Read it each morning next week.\"], \"a\": \"Next Sunday, compare the week with your script and note what matched.\"}, {\"t\": \"Virtual Reality for a Relationship\", \"x\": \"#4\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Seeing a relationship at its best helps you show up that way in real life.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one person you'd like to feel closer to.\", \"Imagine a happy, ordinary scene with them: where, what you say, how they smile.\", \"Stay in the scene 5–10 minutes.\"], \"e\": \"My son and I walk to the park after dinner, he tells me about his day, and we both laugh.\", \"a\": \"Create a small version of that scene this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Creation Box Review\", \"x\": \"#2\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Looking through your box reminds you how much you've already started to receive.\", \"s\": [\"Open your Magical Creation Box from Day 19.\", \"Set aside anything that has arrived, or no longer excites you.\", \"Add 5 new items; with each say, \\\"I'm glad this is on its way.\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Thank someone who helped one of your wishes come true.\"}, {\"t\": \"Prosperity Game: Bigger Numbers\", \"x\": \"#5\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Spending larger pretend amounts stretches your sense of what's possible.\", \"s\": [\"Continue your pretend account, or start again at ₹10,000.\", \"Spend today's deposit fully on paper. Include gifts and giving.\", \"Note how it feels to spend more freely.\"], \"e\": \"₹10,000: new books ₹2,000, a family dinner out ₹3,000, a donation ₹2,000, shoes ₹3,000.\", \"a\": \"Review one real expense you could reduce or one income idea you could try.\"}, {\"t\": \"Wallet Process for a Week\", \"x\": \"#15\", \"m\": \"5 min/day\", \"w\": \"One day showed you the feeling. A week makes \\\"I have enough\\\" your new normal.\", \"s\": [\"Keep your unspent note in your wallet for 7 days.\", \"Each day, mentally spend it at least 10 times.\", \"Each night, write one money moment that felt good.\"], \"a\": \"Start a simple money log: income and spending, 2 minutes a day.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 7 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Notice what is moving towards you.\", \"s\": [\"Compare this week's moods with Week 6.\", \"List any wishes that showed progress this week.\", \"Write: \\\"I'm noticing more ___ in my life.\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Treat yourself to something small you enjoy.\"}, {\"t\": \"Health: Finding the Feeling-Place\", \"x\": \"#18\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Feeling, even briefly, how it is to be well helps you make healthier choices today.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one health wish: more energy, better sleep, less pain.\", \"Imagine one small moment of already having it: waking rested, climbing stairs easily.\", \"Hold that feeling for 3–5 minutes. (Keep following your doctor's advice.)\"], \"a\": \"Do one healthy act that matches the feeling: an early night, a walk, a glass of water.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money: Update Your Debt Columns\", \"x\": \"#19\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Watching the totals fall builds pride and momentum.\", \"s\": [\"Update each column from Day 24 with the new balance.\", \"Draw a line through anything paid off. Celebrate it.\", \"If you have no debt, make columns for savings goals instead.\"], \"a\": \"Make one extra payment, even a small one, or add to savings.\"}, {\"t\": \"Positive Aspects of Yourself in Relationships\", \"x\": \"#8\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"When you like who you are with others, relationships feel easier.\", \"s\": [\"Head a page: \\\"What I bring to my relationships.\\\"\", \"List 15 qualities: listening, humour, loyalty, cooking, patience…\", \"Star 3 you want to show more of this week.\"], \"a\": \"Show one starred quality to someone today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Place Mat for a Week\", \"x\": \"#10\", \"m\": \"10 min/day\", \"w\": \"Handing over what you can't do lightens every day, not just one.\", \"s\": [\"Each morning this week, draw your Place Mat: \\\"I will do today\\\" / \\\"Life will handle.\\\"\", \"Keep the left side short: 3–5 items.\", \"Each evening, tick what got done and note anything that resolved by itself.\"], \"a\": \"Say no to, or postpone, one task that isn't important.\"}, {\"t\": \"Clear a Second Area\", \"x\": \"#14\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Each cleared space brings a little more calm and clarity.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a second area: wardrobe, kitchen shelf, desk or phone photos.\", \"Ask about each item: \\\"Is this important to my life right now?\\\"\", \"Box, donate or delete what isn't. Note where things went.\"], \"a\": \"Give away or donate one bag of items this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Dream Journal\", \"x\": \"#7\", \"m\": \"5 min on waking\", \"w\": \"Your dreams reflect how you've been feeling about things during the day.\", \"s\": [\"Keep a notebook by your bed.\", \"On waking, write how any dream felt: good, bad or neutral.\", \"If it felt bad, find the daytime worry behind it and pivot: \\\"What do I want instead?\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Act on the pivot today in one small way.\"}, {\"t\": \"Set-Point Check\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Your \\\"usual\\\" mood on each subject is your set-point. Seeing it shift shows real change.\", \"s\": [\"For each life area, write the mood number it usually sits at now.\", \"Compare with your Day 1 and Day 30 ratings.\", \"Circle the area that has moved most, and the one that's stuck.\"], \"a\": \"Choose one tool to use on the stuck area next week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Update Your Toolkit\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"After 59 days you know which tools work for you. Write them down.\", \"s\": [\"List your top 5 tools for good days and your top 3 for hard days.\", \"Write each on your toolkit card with one line: \\\"I use it when ___.\\\"\", \"Add your personal rescue steps from Day 28.\"], \"a\": \"Keep the card in your wallet or as your phone wallpaper.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 2 Review\", \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Two-thirds done. See how far you've come before the final phase.\", \"s\": [\"On the Progress tab, fill the Day 60 column.\", \"Compare Day 1, Day 30 and Day 60.\", \"Write the 3 biggest changes since Day 30, and choose ONE big wish for Phase 3.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate 60 days with someone who supports you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Design Your Own Routine\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A routine you designed yourself is one you'll actually keep.\", \"s\": [\"Look at the daily routine checklist. Keep the parts that helped most.\", \"Swap in your favourite tools from your toolkit card.\", \"Write your new routine: morning (5 min), daytime (3 pauses), evening (5 min).\"], \"e\": \"Morning: mood score + 5 appreciations + Segment Intending. Daytime: 3 pivots. Evening: mood score + one Rampage before sleep.\", \"a\": \"Follow your new routine from today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Teach One Idea\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Explaining an idea to someone else is the fastest way to understand it deeply.\", \"s\": [\"Choose the idea that helped you most (e.g. \\\"emotions are a guidance system\\\").\", \"Write how you'd explain it in 3 simple sentences, with one example from your own life.\", \"Share it with a friend or family member.\"], \"a\": \"Ask them what they think, and just listen.\"}, {\"t\": \"Appreciation Without Paper\", \"x\": \"#1\", \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Once appreciation is a habit, you don't need a pen. You can do it anywhere.\", \"s\": [\"While travelling, cooking or waiting, find something to appreciate.\", \"Think about what you like in it for about 17 seconds, then move to the next thing.\", \"Do this at least 5 times today, silently.\"], \"a\": \"Tonight, write the single best moment of appreciation.\"}, {\"t\": \"Real-Time Pivots\", \"x\": \"#16\", \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"The real test is pivoting in the moment, during a conversation or when something goes wrong.\", \"s\": [\"Today, pivot the moment you notice a bad feeling. No writing.\", \"Silently ask: \\\"What do I want?\\\" and hold that thought for a few seconds.\", \"Tonight, count how many times you did it.\"], \"e\": \"A colleague criticises your work → \\\"I want to understand and improve\\\" → you ask a calm question instead of defending.\", \"a\": \"Note the pivot that changed a situation most.\"}, {\"t\": \"Segment Intending for a Hard Week\", \"x\": \"#11\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Planning how you want to feel before a busy or stressful week keeps you steady through it.\", \"s\": [\"List the hardest events of the coming week: deadlines, meetings, family visits.\", \"For each, write: \\\"In this, I want to feel ___ and I want ___ to happen.\\\"\", \"Before each event, reread its line.\"], \"a\": \"Prepare one practical thing for the hardest event today.\"}, {\"t\": \"A No-Complaint Day\", \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Complaining keeps your focus on what you don't want. One day without it shows how much lighter life feels.\", \"s\": [\"Decide: today I won't complain aloud.\", \"When a complaint starts, turn it into \\\"Wouldn't it be nice if…\\\" or a pivot.\", \"If you slip, just start again. No guilt.\"], \"a\": \"Tonight, write how the day felt compared with usual.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 9 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check that your own routine is working for you.\", \"s\": [\"Count the days you followed your new routine this week.\", \"Compare this week's moods with Week 8.\", \"Adjust your routine if any part felt like a chore.\"], \"a\": \"Do something playful just for fun.\"}, {\"t\": \"Choose Your Big Wish\", \"x\": \"#3\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Putting all your tools behind one wish for a week shows you how the whole system works together.\", \"s\": [\"Look at the big wish you chose on Day 60.\", \"Write it in one clear sentence, then add 5 reasons starting \\\"…because.\\\"\", \"Rate how you feel about it now on the Emotional Scale.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I want a job where my skills are valued… because I love solving problems, because I want to grow, because I want to provide well for my family…\\\"\", \"a\": \"Write the wish on a card and keep it with you all week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Focus Wheel + Feeling-Place\", \"x\": \"#17, #18\", \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"The Focus Wheel makes the wish believable; the Feeling-Place lets you feel it as real.\", \"s\": [\"Do a Focus Wheel with your big wish in the centre.\", \"Then close your eyes and imagine one small moment of it being true. Hold it for 3 minutes.\", \"Re-rate your feeling about the wish.\"], \"a\": \"Write the strongest wheel statement on your wish card.\"}, {\"t\": \"Script Its Arrival\", \"x\": \"#9\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A detailed script makes the wish feel close and familiar.\", \"s\": [\"Write one page about the day your wish comes true, in present tense.\", \"Include who you tell, what you feel, and what you do to celebrate.\", \"Read it each night this week.\"], \"a\": \"Do one thing today that the person in your script would do.\"}, {\"t\": \"Inspired Action List\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"The book says action feels easy and natural when it comes from a good-feeling place. Write down the actions that feel inspired, not forced.\", \"s\": [\"Do a 5-minute Rampage of Appreciation first, to feel good.\", \"Then list 10 actions you could take towards your wish. Don't judge them.\", \"Star the ones that feel exciting or easy. Do one today.\"], \"e\": \"Job wish: update my CV ★ · message two old colleagues ★ · take a short online course · ask my manager about new projects ★\", \"a\": \"Do the easiest starred action today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Evidence Log\", \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Noticing small signs that things are moving keeps your belief growing.\", \"s\": [\"Head a page: \\\"Signs my wish is on its way.\\\"\", \"All day, notice anything related: a helpful conversation, an idea, a coincidence, an article.\", \"Write at least 3 tonight, however small.\"], \"a\": \"Follow up on one sign: reply, call, or look into it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Release Resistance\", \"x\": \"#13, #20\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Pushing too hard, or worrying about \\\"how\\\" and \\\"when,\\\" slows things down. Easing off helps.\", \"s\": [\"Write any doubts or worries about your wish.\", \"For each, write a thought that feels a little better (Which Thought Feels Better?).\", \"Then hand over the \\\"how\\\" and \\\"when\\\": list them under \\\"The Manager will handle.\\\"\"], \"e\": \"Doubt: \\\"There aren't good jobs right now.\\\" → \\\"Some people find good jobs every month.\\\" → \\\"I only need one.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do something relaxing and forget the wish for a few hours.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 10 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"See how your feeling about the big wish has changed in one week.\", \"s\": [\"Compare your Day 68 feeling about the wish with today.\", \"Reread your evidence log.\", \"Write: \\\"The tool that helped my big wish most was ___.\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Keep doing one inspired action every few days.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rescue Drill\", \"x\": \"#22\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Practising your rescue steps on a calm day means they work automatically on a bad one.\", \"s\": [\"Remember a recent upsetting moment. Name the feeling and its number.\", \"Use your rescue card: climb the scale one thought at a time.\", \"Time how long it takes to feel some relief.\"], \"a\": \"Improve your rescue card with anything you learned.\"}, {\"t\": \"Repair a Relationship\", \"x\": \"#8, #11\", \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Feeling better about someone first makes a calm, honest talk possible.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a relationship with some tension.\", \"Write 10 positive aspects of the person, then set an intention: \\\"In our next talk, I want us both to feel heard.\\\"\", \"Plan one kind, simple sentence to open the conversation.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I've been thinking about us, and I really value how much you help at home.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Have the talk, or send a warm message, this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money Stress Plan\", \"x\": \"#15, #19\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A ready plan turns money panic into calm action.\", \"s\": [\"Write your 3 most common money worries.\", \"For each, write one feeling tool (e.g. Wallet Process) and one practical step (e.g. call the bank).\", \"Keep this plan with your debt columns.\"], \"e\": \"Worry: \\\"Bills are due before payday.\\\" Feel: Which Thought Feels Better. Act: set up auto-payment dates after payday.\", \"a\": \"Do one practical step from your plan today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Health Worry Plan\", \"x\": \"#21\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Calm thoughts help you follow medical advice and care for your body. This never replaces a doctor.\", \"s\": [\"Write one health worry.\", \"Write the practical step: book an appointment, take medicine on time, ask your doctor a question.\", \"Then do 10 minutes of Reclaiming Natural Health: \\\"It's natural for my body to be well.\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Take the practical step this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Other People's Negativity\", \"x\": \"#12, #16\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"You can't control what others say, but you can choose what you focus on.\", \"s\": [\"Think of someone who often complains or criticises.\", \"Plan 3 easy responses: change the subject, ask \\\"Wouldn't it be nice if…?\\\", or listen kindly without joining in.\", \"Set an intention before you next meet: \\\"I want to stay calm and kind.\\\"\"], \"a\": \"Use one response the next time it happens.\"}, {\"t\": \"Bouncing Back from a Setback\", \"x\": \"#13, #22\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Everyone has setbacks. What matters is how quickly you recover.\", \"s\": [\"Think of a recent setback: a missed day, a bad result, an argument.\", \"Write what happened in 2 lines, without blame.\", \"Write 3 better-feeling thoughts, then one next step.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I missed 4 days of the workbook.\\\" → \\\"I still did 70 days.\\\" → \\\"I know exactly how to restart.\\\" Next step: do today's card.\", \"a\": \"Take the next step today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 11 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"You now have a plan for most hard situations.\", \"s\": [\"List the 5 hard-situation plans you made this week.\", \"Which one have you already used?\", \"Add the best ones to your toolkit card.\"], \"a\": \"Share one plan with someone who could use it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Maintenance Plan\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"After Day 90 there are no daily cards. A simple plan keeps the practice alive.\", \"s\": [\"Choose your minimum daily habit (e.g. mood score + 5 appreciations + 3 pivots).\", \"Choose one weekly tool (e.g. Sunday Focus Wheel or Place Mat).\", \"Choose a monthly check: re-rate your life areas on the 1st of each month.\"], \"a\": \"Put the weekly and monthly checks in your phone calendar now.\"}, {\"t\": \"Habit Stacking\", \"m\": \"10 min\", \"w\": \"Linking a new habit to something you already do makes it automatic.\", \"s\": [\"List 3 things you do every day without fail: brushing teeth, morning tea, commute.\", \"Attach one practice to each: \\\"After my tea, I write 5 appreciations.\\\"\", \"Write the three stacks on a note.\"], \"a\": \"Do all three stacks today.\"}, {\"t\": \"An Appreciation Letter\", \"x\": \"#1\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Telling someone exactly what you appreciate about them lifts both of you.\", \"s\": [\"Choose someone who has helped you, during these 90 days or in life.\", \"Write a letter listing what you appreciate about them, with specific examples.\", \"Give it, send it, or read it to them.\"], \"a\": \"Notice how you both feel afterwards.\"}, {\"t\": \"90 Days of
