\"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"A one-page guide written by you, for you, is the most useful book you'll own.\", \"s\": [\"Head a page \\\"My Guide to Feeling Good.\\\"\", \"Write: my top 5 ideas, my daily routine, my 5 best tools, my rescue steps, and 3 signs I'm slipping.\", \"Keep it at the front of this workbook.\"], \"a\": \"Photograph it and keep it on your phone.\"}, {\"t\": \"Find a tool to them and invite them to try it with you for a week.\", \"Agree on a short weekly check-in.\"], \"a\": \"Send the invitation today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Day 90 Ratings\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Your final ratings show the full journey.\", \"s\": [\"On the Progress tab, fill the Day 90 column for each area.\", \"Rate your usual mood today on the Emotional Scale.\", \"Write one sentence about each area: how it feels now.\"], \"a\": \"Tell someone close the area you're proudest of.\"}, {\"t\": \"Compare Day 1, 30, 60 and 90\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Seeing all four numbers side by side shows you exactly what changed.\", \"s\": [\"Look at your Day 1 / 30 / 60 / 90 ratings on the Progress tab.\", \"Mark the area that improved most and the one that changed least.\", \"Write the small{font-weight:400;color:var(--muted)}\n.moods{display:grid;gap:16px}\nul.checks{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;display:grid;gap:2px}\nul.checks 10px;max-width:100%}\n.res-list{display:grid;gap:8px}\n.res-item{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:12px 14px;background:var(--surface)}\n.res-item Imagine your perfect life with no limits.\", \"s\": [\"Imagine it's five years from today and your life is ideal.\", \"Write a paragraph each for money, family, health and work.\", \"For each, ask: \\\"What would have to happen for this to be true?\\\"\"], \"e\": \"Money: \\\"I earn ₹1.2 lakh a month, own a 2BHK and save ₹20,000 monthly.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do one small thing today toward any part of the picture.\"}, {\"t\": \"Clarify Your Values\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Goals that match your values feel right and last.\", \"s\": [\"List your 3–5 most important values and put them in order.\", \"Write how you'd want to be remembered, in 5 sentences.\", \"Write one behaviour change that fits your values better.\"], \"e\": \"Values: family, honesty, health, learning.\", \"a\": \"Make that one change today.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Ten Goal Exercise\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"The heart of the book: find the one goal that will change your life most.\", \"s\": [\"Write 10 goals in the 3 Ps, each with a deadline.\", \"Ask which one, achieved now, would have the biggest impact. Circle it.\", \"List everything you could do for it; write it on a card to carry.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I work as a senior designer at a top firm, earning ₹1 lakh a month, by 31 December 2027.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take one action from your list today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 1 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Check your direction before you build the plan.\", \"s\": [\"Check your scores on the Progress tab.\", \"Read your Major Definite Purpose aloud. Adjust if needed.\", \"Ask: \\\"What price will I pay for this goal?\\\" Decide to pay it.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Tell one person you trust about your main goal.\"}, {\"t\": \"Check Your Beliefs\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"A self-limiting belief quietly stops you before you start.\", \"s\": [\"List 3 beliefs that might hold you back.\", \"For each, write how you'd act if it were untrue.\", \"Write how a top person in your field thinks and acts differently.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm too old to learn coding\\\" → \\\"I'd start a free online course this week.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Spend one hour today acting as if your new belief were true.\"}, {\"t\": \"Know Your Starting Point\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"To plan a route you need to know exactly where you are now.\", \"s\": [\"Write where you are now on your main goal and how far is left.\", \"Zero-based thinking: list anything you wouldn't start again today.\", \"Write a money snapshot: income, spending, savings, debts.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Start ending one thing from your zero-based list.\"}, {\"t\": \"Make It Measurable\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"What gets measured gets done.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one number that shows progress on your main goal.\", \"Break the goal into 4–6 steps, each with a deadline.\", \"Set a minimum monthly saving amount.\"], \"e\": \"Goal: ₹1 lakh savings by December. Measure: savings balance. ₹20,000 by the end of each month.\", \"a\": \"Do something on the first step today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Remove the Roadblocks\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Find the result areas of your work and rate each 1–10.\", \"Circle your weakest area.\", \"Plan with Three Plus One: read, listen, attend a course, apply.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Start one book, course or video on your weakest area today.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Right People\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"The people around you shape your thinking and your chances.\", \"s\": [\"List 5 important work contacts and 5 personal ones.\", \"Write one way you could help each work contact.\", \"Name one group to join and one person to learn from.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Contact one person today, offering help, not asking for it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 2 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"See what's working and do more of it.\", \"s\": [\"Compare your Week 1 and Week 2 progress scores.\", \"Which action moved you furthest?\", \"How can you do more of it next week?\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Reward yourself for two weeks of daily goal writing.\"}, {\"t\": \"Make a Plan of Action\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"An ordinary person with a plan beats a genius without one.\", \"s\": [\"List every task needed for your main goal.\", \"Number them by priority and mark what \"20% of your tasks bring 80% of your results, so choose what to do first.\", \"s\": [\"List everything for tomorrow.\", \"Mark each task A, B, C, D or E and number the A tasks.\", \"Plan to single-handle your A-1: start it and finish it.\"], \"e\": \"A-1: finish the client proposal. B: emails. D: ask a colleague to book the room. E: reorganise old files.\", \"a\": \"Tomorrow morning, do your A-1 before checking messages.\"}, {\"t\": \"Goal Cards & Daily Review\", \"x\": 15, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Goals you see every day stay alive.\", \"s\": [\"Write each top goal on its own small card in the 3 Ps.\", \"On the back, write 3 actions toward it.\", \"Keep the cards in your wallet or bag.\"], \"e\": \"Front: \\\"I run 5 km without stopping by 1 March.\\\" Back: walk-run 3×/week, buy shoes, join a group.\", \"a\": \"Read your cards morning, noon and night today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Put Your Full Plan Together\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"You've built every piece of a plan. Put it on one page.\", \"s\": [\"Write your goal, why, deadline and measure.\", \"Add sub-goals, main obstacles, skills needed and helpers.\", \"List the first 5 tasks in order.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Photograph the page and keep it on your phone.\"}, {\"t\": \"Visualise Your Goals\", \"x\": 16, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Your imagination is a preview of what's coming.\", \"s\": [\"Picture your main goal as achieved, in detail.\", \"Collect 3–5 pictures of what you want.\", \"Rehearse an important event this week in your mind.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Set one goal picture as your phone wallpaper.\"}, {\"t\": \"Quiet Time for Ideas\", \"x\": 17, \"m\": \"30 min\", \"w\": \"Good ideas often come when the mind is quiet.\", \"s\": [\"Sit alone in silence for 20–30 minutes, no phone.\", \"Let your mind drift; don't force thinking.\", \"Write down any idea about your goal immediately.\"], \"e\": \"Before sleep, ask: \\\"What's the best way to…?\\\" Note what comes on waking.\", \"a\": \"Act on your best idea within 24 hours.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 3 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Habits are forming. Check them.\", \"s\": [\"Count the days you finished your A-1 this week.\", \"Check your goal steps from Day 10. What's done?\", \"Note what to keep and what to change.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Celebrate three weeks: do something you enjoy.\"}, {\"t\": \"Stay Flexible\", \"x\": 18, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Change the method, not the goal.\", \"s\": [\"Ask: \\\"What do I really, really want?\\\" Do my goals match?\", \"Where do I feel resistance? Could I be wrong?\", \"Which step isn't working, and what's a better way?\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Change one thing in your plan that hasn't been working.\"}, {\"t\": \"The 20-Idea Method\", \"x\": 19, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Forcing yourself to 20 answers pushes past the obvious.\", \"s\": [\"Write your goal or problem as \\\"How can I…?\\\"\", \"Write at least 20 answers.\", \"Pick one and do it today.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"How can I earn ₹10,000 more a month?\\\" Ask for a raise, weekend tuition, freelance design…\", \"a\": \"Act on one of your 20 ideas within 24 hours.\"}, {\"t\": \"Best Case, Worst Case, Plan B\", \"x\": 19, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"A backup plan reduces fear and keeps you moving.\", \"s\": [\"Write the best and worst things that could happen in 3 months.\", \"Decide how to reduce the worst and boost the best.\", \"Write a Plan B for your most important area.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Take one step to protect against the worst case.\"}, {\"t\": \"Pick Up the Pace\", \"x\": 20, \"m\": \"15 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Once you're moving fast, it's easier to keep going.\", \"s\": [\"Work as if you leave on holiday tomorrow.\", \"Give each task half the time you'd normally allow.\", \"List everything you finished tonight.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Try getting up 30–60 minutes earlier tomorrow for your main goal.\"}, {\"t\": \"Persist Until You Succeed\", \"x\": 21, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Persistence turns plans into results.\", \"s\": [\"Write your biggest challenge and see it as a test.\", \"List 3 times refusing to give up made the and family life in 5 years.\", \"Write what to do more of and less of from today.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Schedule one family activity and one health habit this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"How Far Have I Come?\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Measure the month.\", \"s\": [\"Open the Progress tab and fill the Day 30 column.\", \"Look at your progress line. What did the best days share?\", \"List everything you did toward your main goal this month.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Share your progress with the person you told on Day 7.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 1 Review: Your Goal System\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"You've built the whole system once. Lock in what works before going deeper.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your 10 goals and circle your Major Definite Purpose.\", \"Decide which daily habits you'll keep: morning rewrite, A-1 first, evening score.\", \"Choose the one goal you'll push hardest in Phase 2 and write why it matters.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate. You finished Phase 1! Tomorrow, Phase 2 begins.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 2 Check-in\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"A clear 30-day target turns your big goal into something you can win this month.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your Major Definite Purpose and its deadline.\", \"Write what \\\"good progress\\\" on it looks like by Day 60, as a number.\", \"Write 3 reasons this goal matters to you.\"], \"e\": \"Goal: ₹1 lakh savings by December. Day 60 target: ₹40,000 saved and a second income idea tested.\", \"a\": \"Write your Day 60 target on a goal card and carry it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Milestones and Deadlines\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A big goal feels lighter when it's broken into small parts, each with its own deadline.\", \"s\": [\"Break your main goal into weekly milestones until Day 60.\", \"Give each milestone a number you can check.\", \"Write each milestone date in your calendar.\"], \"e\": \"Week 5: open a savings account. Week 6: ₹10,000 saved. Week 7: list 3 side-income ideas…\", \"a\": \"Finish this week's milestone, or the first part of it, today.\"}, {\"t\": \"A-1 First, Every Day\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Tracy says starting and finishing your most important task is the habit that matters most.\", \"s\": [\"Last night's ABCDE list: confirm your A-1 for today.\", \"Start it before messages or email.\", \"Stay on it until it's 100% done (single-handling).\"], \"a\": \"Write the time you finished your A-1 today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Block Time for Your Goal\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Important work needs big blocks of time without interruptions.\", \"s\": [\"Look at your week and find three 60–90 minute blocks.\", \"Book them in your calendar as \\\"Goal time.\\\"\", \"Plan what you'll do in each block.\"], \"e\": \"Mon/Wed/Fri 6:00–7:30 am: course lessons and portfolio work.\", \"a\": \"Use your first block this week, with your phone in another room.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Learning Plan\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy's Three Plus One: read, listen, take courses, and apply what you learn quickly.\", \"s\": [\"Choose the one skill that would help your main goal most.\", \"Write a 30-day plan: one book, one audio or video series, one course or workshop.\", \"Decide when you'll learn each day (e.g. 30 minutes before work, audio while travelling).\"], \"a\": \"Start the first lesson today and apply one idea tomorrow.\"}, {\"t\": \"20 Ideas for Your Main Goal\", \"x\": 19, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"The 20-Idea Method works best on the goal you care about most.\", \"s\": [\"Write your main goal as a question: \\\"How can I… by [date]?\\\"\", \"Write 20 answers. Keep going past the obvious ones.\", \"Circle the 3 best and do one today.\"], \"a\": \"Schedule the other two circled ideas this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 5 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check your new daily rhythm.\", \"s\": [\"Count the days you finished your A-1 first this week.\", \"Did you hit this week's milestone? If not, why?\", \"Compare your progress scores with Week 4.\"], \"a\": \"Adjust next week's milestone if it was too big or too small.\"}, {\"t\": \"Replace a Limiting Belief\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"On Day 8 you named your limiting beliefs. Now you replace the strongest one, every day.\", \"s\": [\"Pick the belief that holds you back most.\", \"Write the opposite as a 3P statement (positive, present, personal).\", \"Say it out loud 10 times, morning and night, for a week.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm not a numbers person\\\" → \\\"I understand money and I manage it well.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do one thing today that the new belief would make easy.\"}, {\"t\": \"Find Your Main Constraint\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy says one obstacle usually sets the speed for everything else, and 80% of it is inside you.\", \"s\": [\"List everything slowing your main goal right now.\", \"Mark each as inside you (skill, habit, fear) or outside (money, people, rules).\", \"Circle the single biggest one and write 3 ways to remove it.\"], \"a\": \"Take the easiest of those 3 steps today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Zero-Based Thinking, Round 2\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Ending things that no longer work frees time and energy for your goal.\", \"s\": [\"Ask about each activity, commitment and habit: \\\"Knowing what I know now, would I start this again today?\\\"\", \"List every \\\"no.\\\"\", \"Choose one to end or reduce this week.\"], \"e\": \"An hour of TV each night, a committee you don't care about, a subscription you never use.\", \"a\": \"Cancel, end or cut the one you chose.\"}, {\"t\": \"No Blame, No Excuses Day\", \"x\": 2, \"m\": \"5 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Saying \\\"I am responsible\\\" turns you from a victim into the person in charge.\", \"s\": [\"Today, don't blame anyone or make an excuse, out loud or in your head.\", \"Each time you catch one, say \\\"I am responsible\\\" and ask \\\"What can I do?\\\"\", \"Tonight, count how many you caught.\"], \"a\": \"Act on the best \\\"What can I do?\\\" answer from today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Forgive and Release\", \"x\": 2, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Old anger and guilt keep pulling your attention backwards.\", \"s\": [\"Write the name of one person you still haven't let go of, including yourself.\", \"Write what happened in 3 lines, then: \\\"I forgive you for everything. I let it go.\\\"\", \"Write what you learned from it.\"], \"a\": \"If it's right, send a short, kind message or simply stop retelling the story.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rehearse a Hard Moment\", \"x\": 16, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy suggests rehearsing important events in your mind first, so you perform calmly when they happen.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a coming event that makes you nervous: an interview, a meeting, a sales call.\", \"Close your eyes and see it going perfectly, from start to finish.\", \"Repeat it 3 times, adding detail each time.\"], \"a\": \"Rehearse it once more just before the real event.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 6 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"See which blocks have started to loosen.\", \"s\": [\"How believable is your new belief now, 1–10?\", \"Did you remove or reduce your main constraint?\", \"What did you stop doing, and what did you use the time for?\"], \"a\": \"Tell a supportive person about one block you've cleared.\"}, {\"t\": \"Map Your Key People\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy says your success depends heavily on the people you know and how well you help them.\", \"s\": [\"List the 10 people who matter most to your main goal: boss, clients, colleagues, family, experts.\", \"Next to each, write one way you could help them.\", \"Mark 3 you'll contact this week.\"], \"a\": \"Help one of them today, with nothing asked in return.\"}, {\"t\": \"Learn from the Best\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Spending time with people who are ahead of you changes how you think and what you expect.\", \"s\": [\"Name 2–3 people who've already reached a goal like yours.\", \"Write 3 questions you'd love to ask them.\", \"Find one group, class or community where such people meet.\"], \"e\": \"A local business association, a professional body, an online course community.\", \"a\": \"Ask one of them for 15 minutes of advice, or join the group.\"}, {\"t\": \"Raise Your Hourly Rate\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Knowing what an hour of your time earns shows which tasks are worth doing yourself.\", \"s\": [\"Divide your yearly income by about 2,000 working hours.\", \"List tasks you do that are worth less than that rate.\", \"Choose one to delegate, automate or drop, and one high-value task to do more of.\"], \"e\": \"₹6 lakh a year ÷ 2,000 = ₹300 an hour. Waiting in queues for bill payments? Pay online instead.\", \"a\": \"Delegate or drop one low-value task this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Learn While You Travel\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Tracy recommends turning travel time into learning time with audio programs.\", \"s\": [\"Download one audiobook, podcast or course on your key skill.\", \"Listen during your commute, walk or chores today.\", \"Write one idea you'll apply.\"], \"a\": \"Apply that idea within 24 hours.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Weekly Planning Session\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"30 min\", \"w\": \"Planning the week ahead makes every day start with a clear A-1.\", \"s\": [\"Review last week's milestones and results.\", \"Set next week's milestone and list the tasks for it.\", \"Spread the tasks across the days and book your goal time blocks.\"], \"a\": \"Make this a fixed weekly appointment (e.g. Sunday 7 pm).\"}, {\"t\": \"Solitude and Your Superconscious Mind\", \"x\": 17, \"m\": \"30 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy recommends a daily practice of solitude: sitting quietly and letting your mind relax, so your superconscious mind can bring the ideas you need.\", \"s\": [\"Sit somewhere quiet, with no phone, music or reading.\", \"Let your mind settle. Don't force thoughts.\", \"Write down any idea that comes, especially about your main goal.\"], \"a\": \"Act on the best idea within 24 hours.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 7 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check that people and skills are now working for your goal.\", \"s\": [\"Who did you help or meet this week?\", \"What did you learn, and did you use it?\", \"Compare your progress scores with Week 6.\"], \"a\": \"Send a thank-you to someone who helped you this month.\"}, {\"t\": \"Health: A 30-Day Plan\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Energy and health power every other goal.\", \"s\": [\"Write one health goal in the 3 Ps with a measure and deadline.\", \"Choose 2 daily habits that lead to it (e.g. a 30-minute walk, sleep by 10:30).\", \"Add both habits to your daily checklist.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I walk 8,000 steps every day and weigh 72 kg by 31 January.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do both habits today. (Check with a doctor before big changes.)\"}, {\"t\": \"Family: Time That Counts\", \"x\": 3, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy reminds us that success means a happy life at home as well as at work.\", \"s\": [\"List the people closest to you.\", \"For each, write one thing they'd love more of from you.\", \"Block weekly family time in your calendar, like any important meeting.\"], \"a\": \"Spend one hour of full attention with your family today: no phone.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money: Save and Grow\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"A fixed monthly saving amount turns money goals into a habit.\", \"s\": [\"Update your money snapshot from Day 9: income, spending, savings, debts.\", \"Set or raise your monthly saving amount, and save it first, on payday.\", \"Write one way to raise your income in the next 6 months.\"], \"e\": \"Salary ₹50,000: save ₹5,000 by automatic transfer on the 1st; learn a skill that leads to a raise.\", \"a\": \"Set up or increase the automatic transfer today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Personal Growth: Read One Hour\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"60 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy suggests reading about an hour a day in your field. Try it once and feel the difference.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a book on your key skill.\", \"Read for 60 minutes, notebook open.\", \"Write the 3 best ideas and one action.\"], \"a\": \"Take the action today. Plan when you'll read each day this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Update Your Plan B\", \"x\": 19, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A month of progress changes the risks. Fresh backup plans keep you calm.\", \"s\": [\"For your main goal, write the best and worst cases for the next 3 months.\", \"Update your Plan B for the worst case.\", \"Write one thing that would make the best case more likely.\"], \"a\": \"Do that one thing this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Faster: Half the Time\", \"x\": 20, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"A sense of urgency is what top performers have in common.\", \"s\": [\"List today's tasks with the time you'd usually allow.\", \"Give each one half that time and race the clock.\", \"Tonight, note what you finished early.\"], \"a\": \"Use the time you saved on your main goal.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Goal Scorecard\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"What gets measured gets done. One page shows all your numbers.\", \"s\": [\"Draw a table: each top goal, its measure, the target, where you are now.\", \"Mark each goal on track, behind or ahead.\", \"For any goal behind, write one action to catch up.\"], \"a\": \"Do the catch-up action for the goal furthest behind.\"}, {\"t\": \"Refresh Your Goal List\", \"x\": 18, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Clear on the goal, flexible on the details. Your list should match who you are now.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your 10 goals.\", \"Drop any you no longer want, add any new ones, and adjust deadlines.\", \"Rewrite the final list on fresh goal cards.\"], \"a\": \"Read the new cards morning and night from today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 2 Review\", \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Two-thirds done. See how far you've come before the final phase.\", \"s\": [\"On the Progress tab, fill the Day 60 column.\", \"Did you reach your Day 60 target from Day 31?\", \"Write the 3 biggest changes since Day 30, and choose one big target for a 7-day sprint in Phase 3.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate 60 days with someone who supports you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Design Your Own Routine\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A routine you designed yourself is the one you'll keep.\", \"s\": [\"Look at the daily checklist. Mark the habits that made the biggest difference.\", \"Write your own routine: morning (10 min), daytime, evening (10 min).\", \"Keep it short enough to do on a bad day.\"], \"e\": \"Morning: rewrite top 5 goals + A-1 for today. Daytime: A-1 first, 30 min of goal time. Evening: progress score + ABCDE for tomorrow.\", \"a\": \"Follow your new routine from today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Teach It to Someone\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Explaining the system to someone else is the fastest way to master it.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one tool: the 3 Ps, the Ten Goal Exercise or ABCDE.\", \"Plan how to explain it in 3 simple steps, with your own example.\", \"Teach it to a friend, colleague or family member.\"], \"a\": \"Help them write their first goal in the 3 Ps.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Monthly Planning Ritual\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"30 min\", \"w\": \"A monthly check keeps every goal moving, not just the main one.\", \"s\": [\"Look back: what did you achieve this month in each life area?\", \"Look ahead: set one target per area for next month.\", \"Put the monthly ritual in your calendar for the 1st of each month.\"], \"a\": \"Do the first task for next month's main target today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Revisit Your 5-Year Vision\", \"x\": 3, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"After two months of action your picture of the future may have grown.\", \"s\": [\"Reread what you wrote on Day 4.\", \"Rewrite your ideal life 5 years from now, bigger and clearer.\", \"Write the one goal for the next 12 months that moves you most toward it.\"], \"a\": \"Add that 12-month goal to your goal cards.\"}, {\"t\": \"Values Check\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Goals that fight your values cause stress, even when you reach them.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your values from Day 5.\", \"For each top goal, ask: \\\"Does this fit what I stand for?\\\"\", \"Change any goal or method that doesn't fit.\"], \"a\": \"Do one thing today that shows your top value in action.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Single-Handling Day\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Tracy calls single-handling one of the most powerful time-management techniques of all: start a task and stay on it until it's 100% complete. When you're tempted to stop, tell yourself: \\\"Back to work!\\\"\", \"s\": [\"Choose your 3 most important tasks today.\", \"Do them one at a time. Don't switch until each is 100% finished.\", \"Turn off notifications while you work.\"], \"a\": \"Tonight, note how much you finished compared with a normal day.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 9 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check that your own system works for you.\", \"s\": [\"Count the days you followed your new routine.\", \"Compare your progress scores with Week 8.\", \"Simplify any part of the routine that felt like a chore.\"], \"a\": \"Do something relaxing just for you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Choose Your Sprint Target\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Pushing one target hard for a week shows how fast the whole system can move.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one part of your main goal you can finish or clearly advance in 7 days.\", \"Write it in the 3 Ps with a measure and the date 7 days from now.\", \"Write why finishing it matters.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"By next Sunday I have sent my updated CV to 10 companies and booked 2 interviews.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Tell one person about your sprint target.\"}, {\"t\": \"Plan the Sprint\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A plan sorted by priority and sequence makes every day of the sprint clear.\", \"s\": [\"List every task the sprint needs.\", \"Put them in order: what comes first, what matters most.\", \"Give each task a day this week and book the time.\"], \"a\": \"Do the first task today.\"}, {\"t\": \"See It Done\", \"x\": 16, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Seeing the result clearly, again and again, keeps your energy high all week.\", \"s\": [\"Close your eyes and picture the sprint finished: what you see, hear and feel.\", \"Write a 3P statement of the result and read it morning and night.\", \"Rehearse the hardest step in your mind.\"], \"a\": \"Do the hardest step first today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Deep-Work Day\", \"x\": 20, \"m\": \"2 hours\", \"w\": \"Two focused hours often achieve more than a scattered day.\", \"s\": [\"Book 2 hours with no phone, email or interruptions.\", \"Work only on sprint tasks, single-handling each one.\", \"Write what you finished.\"], \"a\": \"Book another 2-hour block before the sprint ends.\"}, {\"t\": \"Clear the Next Roadblock\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Every sprint hits an obstacle. Find it early and deal with it.\", \"s\": [\"Write what's slowing the sprint right now.\", \"Ask: \\\"What's the real problem? What else is the problem?\\\"\", \"Choose the best solution and act on it.\"], \"a\": \"Remove or get around the roadblock today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Ask for Help\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Someone you know can probably save you days of effort.\", \"s\": [\"Write the one thing someone else could help you with in this sprint.\", \"Choose the best person to ask.\", \"Ask clearly, and offer something in return.\"], \"a\": \"Send the request today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Sprint Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Look at what a focused week produced.\", \"s\": [\"Did you hit your sprint target? Score it 0–10.\", \"What helped most, and what got in the way?\", \"Write what you'll repeat in your normal weeks.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate the sprint, whatever the score.\"}, {\"t\": \"Find the Lesson\", \"x\": 21, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Tracy teaches that every setback holds a valuable lesson or benefit, if you look for it.\", \"s\": [\"Write your biggest recent setback in 2 lines, without blame.\", \"Ask: \\\"What is the lesson here? What is the benefit?\\\" Write at least 3 answers.\", \"Write what you'll do differently next time.\"], \"e\": \"Lost a client → lesson: I didn't follow up for a month → new rule: a short check-in every 2 weeks.\", \"a\": \"Apply the lesson in one action today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Plan B Drill\", \"x\": 19, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Practising your backup plan on a calm day means you act fast when trouble comes.\", \"s\": [\"Imagine your main plan suddenly stops working.\", \"Read your Plan B. Is it still realistic?\", \"Write the first 3 steps you'd take tomorrow if you had to use it.\"], \"a\": \"Do one small thing now that makes Plan B easier later.\"}, {\"t\": \"Face One Fear\", \"x\": 21, \"m\": \"15 min + action\", \"w\": \"Tracy says courage grows when you do the thing you fear.\", \"s\": [\"Write the one action for your goal that you've been avoiding out of fear.\", \"Write the worst thing that could realistically happen, and how you'd handle it.\", \"Decide when you'll do it.\"], \"e\": \"Asking for a raise, calling a big client, showing your work in public.\", \"a\": \"Do it, or take the first step, within 24 hours.\"}, {\"t\": \"When Motivation Drops\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Motivation comes and goes; your reasons don't.\", \"s\": [\"Reread why you want your main goal (Day 31).\", \"Picture the goal achieved for 2 minutes.\", \"Write the smallest possible step you can take today, even 10 minutes.\"], \"a\": \"Take that small step. Momentum will follow.\"}, {\"t\": \"Too Much to Do\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Overload usually means too many C, D and E tasks.\", \"s\": [\"List everything on your plate this week.\", \"Mark each A, B, C, D or E.\", \"Delegate the Ds, cross out the Es, and say no to one new request.\"], \"a\": \"Hand over or drop at least two tasks today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Restart After Missed Days\", \"m\": \"10 min\", \"w\": \"Everyone misses days. 10px;max-width:100%}\n.res-list{display:grid;gap:8px}\n.res-item{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:12px 14px;background:var(--surface)}\n.res-item b{display:block;margin-bottom:2px}\n.callout{border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--accent-soft);border-radius:8px;padding:12px text{font-family:var(--f-body)}\ntable.areas{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.92rem}\ntable.areas th,table.areas td{padding:8px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid Today that includes phones and shows.\", \"s\": [\"Check your screen-time report for yesterday.\", \"Set a 60-minute limit for entertainment screens today.\", \"Use the saved time for one big-thinking task.\"], \"a\": \"Turn on a daily limit in your phone settings.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Gossip-Free Week\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"5 min + 7 days\", \"w\": \"Gossip is thought poison. Looking your best helps you think and act your best.\", \"s\": [\"Before you leave home, do a careful check: clothes, grooming, shoes.\", \"Fix one thing you usually skip.\", \"Notice how you stand and speak today.\"], \"a\": \"Make the morning check part of your daily routine.\"}, {\"t\": \"Plan Tomorrow Tonight\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"10 min\", \"w\": \"Planning each day's work the night before is one of the habits on the book's improvement guide.\", \"s\": [\"Tonight, list tomorrow's tasks.\", \"Mark the 3 that matter most.\", \"Decide the first thing you'll do in the morning.\"], \"a\": \"Do it every night this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Compliment People\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Everyone craves praise. --r:10px;\n}\n@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark){:root:not([data-theme=\"light\"]){\n  --bg:#13152a; --surface:#1c1f38; --ink:#e8e9f4; --muted:#a3a7c4; --line:#2e3254;\n  --accent:#c4abf2; color-scheme:dark}\n*{box-sizing:border-box}\n[hidden]{display:none!important}\nbody{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:16px/1.55 Those words became beliefs.\", \"s\": [\"Write 5 things you were often told as a child (good or bad).\", \"Next to each, write how it still shows up in your life.\", \"Circle the ones that no longer serve you.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"You're careless\\\" → I still double-check everything and doubt myself.\", \"a\": \"Keep listening; add 5 more caught phrases.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Self-Management Sequence\", \"x\": 3, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Programming leads to beliefs, then attitudes, feelings, behaviour and results. you'll rewrite it next week.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I can't…\\\", \\\"Nothing ever works out\\\", \\\"It's not my day\\\"\", \"a\": \"Each time you hear one today, put a tick next to it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Media Diet\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"15 min + 7 days\", \"w\": \"TV, news, ads and social media programme you too.\", \"s\": [\"List what media you took in today.\", \"Note how it made you feel or what it made you want.\", \"Ask: \\\"Who's in control here?\\\"\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Cut one negative media source for the next week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 1 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"You now know what you've been telling yourself.\", \"s\": [\"Check your scores on the Progress tab.\", \"Count the ticks on your top-10 list: which phrase wins?\", \"Write one sentence: what surprised you most?\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Tell a friend one phrase you're going to stop saying.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Five Levels\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Not all self-talk is equal. Levels I and II work against you; III and IV work for you.\", \"s\": [\"Learn the levels: I can't / I should / I no longer / I am / It is.\", \"Label each of your top-10 phrases I or II.\", \"Notice how many are \\\"I should\\\" (Level II).\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I really need to exercise\\\" is Level II: it names the problem but changes nothing.\", \"a\": \"Catch 3 Level I or II phrases today and name their level.\"}, {\"t\": \"Escape the \\\"I Should\\\" Trap\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"\\\"I should\\\" feels positive but keeps you stuck. Level III states the decision as already made.\", \"s\": [\"List 5 \\\"I should\\\" or \\\"I need to\\\" phrases you use.\", \"Rewrite each as Level III: \\\"I no longer…\\\" or \\\"I never…\\\".\", \"Say them out loud.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I should stop eating junk food\\\" → \\\"I no longer eat junk food.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Act on one Level III statement today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rewrite as \\\"I Am\\\"\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Level IV paints the complete new picture of you. It's the most effective self-talk.\", \"s\": [\"Rewrite all 10 phrases from Day 5 as Level IV (\\\"I am…\\\", \\\"I do…\\\").\", \"Check each against the 7-point checklist.\", \"Choose your best 5.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I can never remember names\\\" → \\\"I have a great memory for names. People matter to me.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Read your best 5 aloud three times today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Self-Write\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Writing your new self-talk makes it visible all day.\", \"s\": [\"Write your 5 statements on cards or sticky notes.\", \"Put them on your mirror, desk and phone lock screen.\", \"Rewrite them from memory tomorrow morning.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Read each card every time you see it today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Self-Conversation\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Talking to yourself out loud, like a coach, is one of the book's most activating methods.\", \"s\": [\"Stand at a mirror or go for a walk.\", \"Talk to yourself by name, out loud, using your statements.\", \"Encourage yourself as you would a friend.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"Anil, you're organised. You plan your day and you get it done. Today's going to be good.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do a 2-minute self-conversation before your hardest task.\"}, {\"t\": \"Tape-Talk: Record It\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"The author calls listening to recorded self-talk the most effective method. Your phone makes it easy.\", \"s\": [\"Record your statements as a 2–3 minute voice memo, in your own voice.\", \"Listen once now.\", \"Plan to listen every morning and every night.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Listen to your recording twice today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 2 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"You've learned to rewrite. Now it needs repetition.\", \"s\": [\"Compare your Week 1 and Week 2 levels.\", \"Which method do you enjoy most: cards, out loud, recording?\", \"Improve one statement that doesn't feel right.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Reward yourself for two weeks of practice.\"}, {\"t\": \"Change a Habit\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"To change the habit, change the words.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one habit to change.\", \"Write 5 Level IV statements about the new habit.\", \"Add them to your recording.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I enjoy getting up early. I wake rested and ready. My mornings are calm and productive.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do the new habit once today, saying your statement as you do it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Change an Attitude\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Attitudes are habits of thinking, and they change with new words.\", \"s\": [\"Pick one attitude you'd like to change (about work, people, money).\", \"Write 5 Level IV statements for the new attitude.\", \"Check them with the 7-point checklist.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I like the people I work with. I look for the best in them.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Act on the new attitude once today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Watch Your Self-Speak\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"All day\", \"w\": \"What you say out loud to others programmes you, and them.\", \"s\": [\"Notice every complaint or negative opinion you say out loud.\", \"Replace each with a neutral or positive statement.\", \"Count how many you caught.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"This weather is awful\\\" → \\\"Good day for chai indoors.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Go a full meal-time without a single complaint.\"}, {\"t\": \"Self-Talk for Problems\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Problems feel bigger when your self-talk says \\\"I can't handle this\\\".\", \"s\": [\"Name one current problem.\", \"Write 5 statements that describe you solving it.\", \"Write the first action your new self would take.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm calm and clear. I find answers. I handle this one step at a time.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take that first action today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Self-Talk for Goals\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Goals stick when your self-talk describes you as already reaching them.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one goal.\", \"Write 5–6 Level IV statements about achieving it, safely and healthily.\", \"Add them to your recording.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I save ₹5,000 every month. I enjoy watching my savings grow.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do one thing toward the goal today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Morning Positive Push\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Real motivation comes from inside, from the words you give yourself each morning.\", \"s\": [\"Write a 1-minute morning script about who you are today.\", \"Record it.\", \"Play it the moment you wake up.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"Today is a good day. I'm energetic and focused. I handle whatever comes.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Play it tomorrow before you check your phone.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 3 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Habits, attitudes, problems and goals now have scripts.\", \"s\": [\"Check your levels this week.\", \"Which script made the most difference?\", \"Re-record your recording with your best lines.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Celebrate three weeks: do something you enjoy.\"}, {\"t\": \"Situational Self-Talk\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Ready-made words for daily triggers change how you respond in the moment.\", \"s\": [\"List 5 daily situations that upset you.\", \"Write one short phrase for each.\", \"Memorise them.\"], \"e\": \"Before cooking: \\\"This is going to be fun.\\\" After: \\\"That wasn't so bad!\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use at least two phrases today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Instant Adjustments\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"All day\", \"w\": \"Situational self-talk can't change the problem, but it changes your reaction.\", \"s\": [\"When something goes wrong, pause.\", \"Say your situational phrase.\", \"Notice how the rest of the day goes.\"], \"e\": \"Queue: \\\"A few minutes to breathe and think.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Note every adjustment you made tonight.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money Self-Talk\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"How you talk about money shapes how you handle it.\", \"s\": [\"Write 3 things you often say about money.\", \"Rewrite them as Level IV.\", \"Add them to your recording.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm in control of my money. I spend wisely and save every month.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take one small money action: save, track or plan.\"}, {\"t\": \"Health Self-Talk\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Words like \\\"I'm always tired\\\" programme your body's habits too.\", \"s\": [\"Write 3 things you say about your body or health.\", \"Rewrite them as Level IV.\", \"Add one healthy action to each.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I take good care of my body. I walk every day and sleep well.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Do one healthy action today. (Self-talk supports medical care; it never replaces it.)\"}, {\"t\": \"Self-Talk for Relationships\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"What we say to others, especially children, becomes their self-talk.\", \"s\": [\"Write 3 things you often say to people close to you.\", \"Rewrite them as encouraging statements.\", \"Write one Level IV statement about the relationship.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"You never listen\\\" → \\\"I love it when we talk. Tell me more.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use one new encouraging sentence with someone today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Master Script\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Combine your best lines into one recording you'll keep using.\", \"s\": [\"Pick your best 10–15 statements from all areas.\", \"Order them: self, health, work, money, relationships.\", \"Record a 5-minute master script.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Listen to your master script morning and night from now on.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Rescue Card\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"For bad days, have your best lines ready.\", \"s\": [\"Write your 3 strongest Level IV statements.\", \"Write your 3 best situational phrases.\", \"Copy them onto a wallet card.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Read the card at your next difficult moment.\"}, {\"t\": \"How Far Have I Come?\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Measure the month.\", \"s\": [\"Open the Progress tab and fill the Day 30 column.\", \"Look at your level line over 30 days.\", \"Check your Day 5 list. Which phrases have faded?\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Share one change with someone who supports you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 1 Review: Choose to Change\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Phase 1 is done. The book ends with a choice: keep the old programming, or keep reprogramming.\", \"s\": [\"Decide which daily habits you'll keep: cards, recording, situational phrases.\", \"Pick one area to go deeper on in Phase 2.\", \"Write and record a script for it.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate. You finished Phase 1! Tomorrow, Phase 2 begins.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 2 Check-in\", \"x\": 2, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"After a month, some old phrases have faded and new ones may have appeared. A fresh list keeps the work real.\", \"s\": [\"Write today's 10 most-used negative phrases, without looking at Day 5.\", \"Compare with your Day 5 list. Tick the ones that are gone.\", \"Circle the 3 that still come back most often.\"], \"a\": \"Rewrite the 3 circled phrases as Level IV tonight.\"}, {\"t\": \"Master Script 2.0\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Your recording should match who you're becoming now, not who you were a month ago.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your Day 27 master script.\", \"Replace weak lines and add your 3 new Level IV statements.\", \"Re-record it in a calm, confident voice (3–5 minutes).\"], \"a\": \"Listen to it tonight before sleep.\"}, {\"t\": \"Listen Three Times\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"The author found that repetition is what turns new self-talk into new programming.\", \"s\": [\"Listen to your recording on waking.\", \"Listen again while travelling, walking or doing chores.\", \"Listen a third time before sleep.\"], \"a\": \"Set a reminder for the middle listen so you don't miss it.\"}, {\"t\": \"The \\\"Stop\\\" Drill\", \"x\": 5, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Silent self-talk runs all day. Catching it fast is the skill that makes everything else work.\", \"s\": [\"Each time you catch a negative thought, say \\\"Stop\\\" in your mind.\", \"Replace it at once with a Level IV sentence.\", \"Keep a tally of how many you caught.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'll mess this up\\\" → \\\"Stop.\\\" → \\\"I'm prepared and I handle this well.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Tonight, write your tally and your best replacement.\"}, {\"t\": \"Mirror Self-Conversation\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"10 min\", \"w\": \"Speaking to yourself out loud, by name, with energy, makes the new words feel real.\", \"s\": [\"Stand at a mirror for 5 minutes.\", \"Talk to yourself by name about the day ahead, using Level IV statements.\", \"Speak firmly, like a coach who believes in you.\"], \"a\": \"Do 1 more minute of self-conversation before your hardest task.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Self-Talk Journal Page\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Writing your self-talk as a full page builds a clearer picture than single lines.\", \"s\": [\"Write one page in present tense about yourself at your best.\", \"Cover how you think, act, speak and feel.\", \"Underline the 5 strongest sentences.\"], \"a\": \"Copy the 5 underlined sentences onto fresh cards.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 5 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check how often the new words now come on their own.\", \"s\": [\"Compare this week's levels with Week 4.\", \"How many times did you listen to your recording this week?\", \"Which old phrase is weakest now?\"], \"a\": \"Tell someone one phrase you no longer use.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Second Habit\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"The habit from Day 15 has had three weeks of new words. Now start a second one.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a second habit you want to change.\", \"Write 5 Level IV statements that describe the new habit as already yours.\", \"Add them to your master recording.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I put my phone away at 10 pm. I sleep deeply and wake fresh.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Practise the new habit once today, saying a statement as you do it.\"}, {\"t\": \"Link the Habit to a Trigger\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Every habit has a moment that sets it off. A ready phrase at that moment changes your response.\", \"s\": [\"Write the exact moment the old habit usually starts.\", \"Write a short situational phrase for that moment.\", \"Say it out loud the next three times the moment comes.\"], \"e\": \"Opening the fridge at night → \\\"I'm satisfied. I'll have water and go to bed.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Tonight, note whether the phrase changed what you did.\"}, {\"t\": \"Time and Procrastination\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"\\\"I'll do it later\\\" is self-talk too, and it can be rewritten.\", \"s\": [\"Write the phrases you use when you put things off.\", \"Rewrite each as Level IV.\", \"Choose one task you've been avoiding.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm too busy\\\" → \\\"I do the important things first, and I get them done.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Start the avoided task for 10 minutes, saying your new line.\"}, {\"t\": \"Body and Energy\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"What you tell yourself about food, exercise and energy shapes what you do. (Self-talk supports medical care; it never replaces it.)\", \"s\": [\"Write 3 things you say about your body, eating or exercise.\", \"Rewrite them as Level IV.\", \"Add them to your recording.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I hate exercise\\\" → \\\"I enjoy moving my body. A walk clears my head.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take one healthy step today: a walk, water, an early night.\"}, {\"t\": \"Money Habits\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Spending and saving follow the words you use about money.\", \"s\": [\"List 3 money habits you want to change.\", \"Write a Level IV statement for each.\", \"Put one on a card in your wallet.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I check the price and ask: do I need this?\\\" · \\\"I save first, then spend.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Read the wallet card before every purchase today.\"}, {\"t\": \"A No-Complaint Day\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Every complaint you say out loud is self-speak that reinforces the old program.\", \"s\": [\"Decide: today I won't complain out loud.\", \"When a complaint starts, change it to a neutral or positive statement.\", \"If you slip, start again. No guilt.\"], \"a\": \"Tonight, write how the day felt compared with usual.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 6 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Habits change slowly. Look for small signs.\", \"s\": [\"For each habit you worked on, write one change you've noticed.\", \"Which trigger phrase worked best?\", \"Compare this week's levels with Week 5.\"], \"a\": \"Reward yourself for one habit that has started to shift.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Attitude to Work\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"How you talk about your work (or studies) shapes how you feel doing it every day.\", \"s\": [\"Write the 3 things you say most about your work.\", \"Rewrite each as Level IV.\", \"Check them with the 7-point checklist.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"My job is boring\\\" → \\\"I find something to learn in my work every day.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Say one of the new lines before you start work tomorrow.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Attitude to People\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"What you tell yourself about someone colours every meeting with them.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a person you find difficult.\", \"Write what you usually think about them.\", \"Write 3 fair, Level IV statements about how you deal with them.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"He always criticises me\\\" → \\\"I stay calm and listen for what's useful.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use one statement before your next meeting with them.\"}, {\"t\": \"What You Say to Your Family\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"The author stresses that what we say to children and family becomes their self-talk.\", \"s\": [\"Write 5 things you often say to your children, partner or parents.\", \"Mark each as helping or hurting.\", \"Rewrite the hurting ones as encouraging statements.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"Why are you so lazy?\\\" → \\\"I know you can finish this. Let's start together.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use two new encouraging sentences at home today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Listen to Others Again\", \"x\": 1, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Now that you know the Five Levels, you'll hear other people's programming clearly.\", \"s\": [\"Listen to the self-talk of 3 people today.\", \"Notice what level each mostly speaks at.\", \"Notice how you feel after time with each.\"], \"a\": \"Spend more time with the person who speaks at the highest level.\"}, {\"t\": \"Media Check, Round 2\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"15 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Media keeps programming you, even when you aren't paying attention.\", \"s\": [\"List the media you took in today: news, shows, social feeds, ads.\", \"For each, ask: \\\"Who's in control here?\\\"\", \"Replace one negative source with something that supports your new self-talk.\"], \"a\": \"Unfollow or mute one source that leaves you feeling worse.\"}, {\"t\": \"Self-Image Script\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"How you describe yourself is the self-talk behind all your other self-talk.\", \"s\": [\"Write 8 Level IV statements about who you are: qualities, strengths, values.\", \"Make them specific and believable.\", \"Record them at the start of your master script.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm a person who keeps promises. I learn fast. I treat people well.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Read them out loud at the mirror tonight.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 7 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"See how your words are changing your relationships.\", \"s\": [\"What changed at home or at work after your new sentences?\", \"Which person's self-talk affected you most this week?\", \"Compare this week's levels with Week 6.\"], \"a\": \"Thank someone whose words lift you up.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Problem Script\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A problem you keep describing as impossible stays impossible in your mind.\", \"s\": [\"Choose the problem that worries you most right now.\", \"Write 6 Level IV statements of you handling it well.\", \"Add the first practical step at the end.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm clear-headed about this. I find the facts. I ask for help when I need it. I take one step today.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take the first step today.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Goal Script\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"A goal described in present tense, as already happening, gives your mind a clear target.\", \"s\": [\"Choose your most important goal for the next 60 days.\", \"Write 6–8 Level IV statements about achieving it.\", \"Check each against the 7-point checklist, especially \\\"safe and healthy\\\" and \\\"asks enough of me\\\".\"], \"a\": \"Add the goal script to your recording and listen tonight.\"}, {\"t\": \"Morning Push 2.0\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"The book calls outside motivation a myth: it fades. Motivation that lasts comes from your own words each morning.\", \"s\": [\"Rewrite your Day 20 morning script with your new goal and self-image lines.\", \"Keep it to about one minute.\", \"Record it and set it as your first listen of the day.\"], \"a\": \"Play it before you look at your phone tomorrow.\"}, {\"t\": \"An Evening Wind-Down Script\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"The last words before sleep can be calm and positive instead of worries.\", \"s\": [\"Write 5 calm Level IV lines about the day ending and tomorrow.\", \"Record them slowly, in a soft voice.\", \"Play them in bed tonight.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I did my best today. I let the day go. I sleep deeply and wake ready.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use it every night this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Situational Set: Work\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Work brings the same triggers again and again. Prepare a phrase for each.\", \"s\": [\"List 5 work triggers: a deadline, a meeting, an email, a difficult customer, a mistake.\", \"Write one short phrase for each.\", \"Save them on your phone.\"], \"e\": \"Before a meeting: \\\"I'm prepared and I speak clearly.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use at least two today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Situational Set: Home\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Home has its own triggers: rushed mornings, chores, arguments.\", \"s\": [\"List 5 home triggers.\", \"Write one short phrase for each.\", \"Share one with your family.\"], \"e\": \"Morning rush: \\\"We have time. One thing at a time.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use at least two today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Check All Your Scripts\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Two months of scripts need a quality check.\", \"s\": [\"Gather every script and card you've written.\", \"Run each line through the 7-point checklist.\", \"Fix or remove any line that fails.\"], \"a\": \"Rewrite your cards with only the strongest lines.\"}, {\"t\": \"Re-record Your Master Script\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A fresh recording, with checked lines, keeps your listening sessions strong.\", \"s\": [\"Order your best lines: self-image, habits, health, work, money, relationships, goal.\", \"Record 5 minutes in a confident voice.\", \"Delete older recordings so you only hear the new one.\"], \"a\": \"Listen twice today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 2 Review\", \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Two-thirds done. See how far you've come before the final phase.\", \"s\": [\"On the Progress tab, fill the Day 60 column.\", \"Compare Day 1, Day 30 and Day 60.\", \"Write the 3 biggest changes since Day 30, and choose one big change to focus on in Phase 3.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate 60 days with someone who supports you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Design Your Own Routine\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A routine you designed yourself is the one you'll keep.\", \"s\": [\"Look at the daily checklist. Mark the parts that helped most.\", \"Write your own short routine: morning, daytime, evening.\", \"Keep it simple enough for a busy day.\"], \"e\": \"Morning: morning push + one card out loud. Daytime: \\\"Stop\\\" drill and one situational phrase. Evening: wind-down recording.\", \"a\": \"Follow your new routine from today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Teach the Five Levels\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Explaining the levels to someone else makes you hear your own self-talk even more clearly.\", \"s\": [\"Plan a simple explanation of the Five Levels with one example of each.\", \"Share it with a friend or family member.\", \"Help them rewrite one of their own phrases as Level IV.\"], \"a\": \"Agree to point out each other's Level I phrases for a week, kindly.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Level III to IV Upgrade\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Level III (\\\"I no longer…\\\") helps, but Level IV (\\\"I am…\\\") paints the full picture.\", \"s\": [\"Find every Level III line in your scripts and cards.\", \"Upgrade each to Level IV.\", \"Read the upgraded lines out loud.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I no longer get angry in traffic\\\" → \\\"I'm calm and patient on the road.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Add the upgrades to your recording.\"}, {\"t\": \"Self-Talk for Learning\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"\\\"I'm not good at this\\\" stops learning before it starts.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a skill you're learning or want to learn.\", \"Write 5 Level IV lines about learning it.\", \"Spend 20 minutes practising the skill today.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I learn new things easily. Each try makes me better. I enjoy getting it right.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Say a line before each practice session this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Level IV Day\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"One full day of speaking only at Level IV, out loud and in your head, shows how far you've come.\", \"s\": [\"Decide: today I speak about myself only at Level IV.\", \"Catch and rewrite every slip.\", \"Tonight, count your slips.\"], \"a\": \"Note the moment that was hardest, and write a phrase for it.\"}, {\"t\": \"The \\\"Can't\\\" Swap at Home\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Turning the practice into a family game makes good self-talk normal at home.\", \"s\": [\"Explain the game: when anyone says \\\"I can't\\\", they rephrase it.\", \"Play it at home for the day.\", \"Notice who catches the most.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I can't do maths\\\" → \\\"I'm getting better at maths every week.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Praise one family member for a great rewrite.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 9 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check that your own routine is working.\", \"s\": [\"Count the days you followed your new routine.\", \"Compare this week's levels with Week 8.\", \"Simplify any part that felt like a chore.\"], \"a\": \"Do something relaxing just for you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Choose Your Big Change\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Focusing all your self-talk tools on one change for a week shows what the method can picture of the new you in this one area.\", \"s\": [\"Write 10 Level IV lines: how you think, feel, act and speak in this area.\", \"Check each against the 7-point checklist.\", \"Read the whole script out loud.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I prepare one point before every meeting. I speak early. My voice is steady. People listen to my ideas…\\\"\", \"a\": \"Copy your 3 strongest lines onto a card.\"}, {\"t\": \"Record and Listen Three Times\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Concentrated repetition on one area speeds up the new programming.\", \"s\": [\"Record your 10-line script.\", \"Listen morning, midday and night.\", \"Keep doing this every day this week.\"], \"a\": \"Set three phone reminders for your listens.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phrases for the Moment\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"The big change will be tested in specific moments. Prepare the words for each.\", \"s\": [\"List the 3 moments where this change matters most.\", \"Write a short situational phrase for each.\", \"Rehearse saying them.\"], \"e\": \"Just before speaking: \\\"My idea is worth hearing.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use one phrase in a real moment today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Coach Yourself Out Loud\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"10 min\", \"w\": \"Self-conversation before the moment gives you energy and clarity.\", \"s\": [\"Before a key moment, talk to yourself by name for 2 minutes.\", \"Describe exactly how you'll handle it, at Level IV.\", \"Afterwards, tell yourself what went well.\"], \"a\": \"Do it before at least one real event today.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Evidence Log\", \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Noticing proof that the change is happening strengthens the new belief.\", \"s\": [\"Head a page \\\"Proof I'm changing.\\\"\", \"Write every small sign today: something you did, said or felt differently.\", \"Aim for at least 3.\"], \"a\": \"Read the evidence log before sleep.\"}, {\"t\": \"Big Change Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"See what one focused week produced.\", \"s\": [\"Rate the change 1–10 now, and compare with how you'd have rated it on Day 68.\", \"Which tool helped most: recording, phrases, or coaching?\", \"Decide whether to keep this focus for another week.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate the progress, however small.\"}, {\"t\": \"When You're Criticised\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Criticism can trigger old Level I self-talk instantly. Have your words ready.\", \"s\": [\"Remember a recent criticism and what you told yourself.\", \"Write a short phrase for the moment of criticism.\", \"Write a Level IV line for afterwards.\"], \"e\": \"In the moment: \\\"I listen and learn what's useful.\\\" Afterwards: \\\"I'm open to feedback and I keep improving.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Add both lines to your rescue card.\"}, {\"t\": \"After a Setback\", \"x\": 3, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A setback is when old programming is loudest. Trace it and rewrite it.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a recent setback.\", \"Trace it with the Self-Management Sequence: what did you tell yourself?\", \"Write 3 Level IV lines for next time.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I always fail at interviews\\\" → \\\"I learn from every interview. I'm better prepared each time.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Take one practical step toward trying again.\"}, {\"t\": \"When You Feel Anxious\", \"x\": 5, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Worry is often silent self-talk on repeat. Calm words can slow it down. (If anxiety is strong or lasting, please talk to a doctor or counsellor.)\", \"s\": [\"Write the worried thoughts that come most often.\", \"For each, write a calm Level IV line.\", \"Practise: catch, \\\"Stop\\\", replace, and take three slow breaths.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"What if it all goes wrong?\\\" → \\\"I'm prepared. I handle what comes, one step at a time.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use the routine the next time worry starts.\"}, {\"t\": \"When You're Angry\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Anger grows with the words you repeat about what happened.\", \"s\": [\"Remember a recent angry moment and what you said to yourself.\", \"Write a short phrase to pause the anger.\", \"Write a Level IV line about how you handle anger.\"], \"e\": \"Pause phrase: \\\"Breathe. Decide later.\\\" Level IV: \\\"I stay calm and say what I need clearly.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use the pause phrase at the next sign of anger.\"}, {\"t\": \"Low-Energy Days\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"On tired days, the old words come back first. A short recording helps.\", \"s\": [\"Write 5 short lines for days when you feel flat.\", \"Record them as a 1-minute \\\"low-energy\\\" track.\", \"Note one small thing you can always do on a bad day.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I take it easy and still take one step. Small steps count. Tomorrow I feel fresh.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Save the track where you can find it fast. (Check with a doctor if tiredness lasts.)\"}, {\"t\": \"When Old Habits Return\", \"x\": \"3, 9\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Old programming can return under stress. That's normal. The answer is more repetition, not giving up.\", \"s\": [\"Name the old habit or phrase that came back.\", \"Find the trigger and write a fresh phrase for it.\", \"Listen to your recording twice today.\"], \"a\": \"Write your restart card: 3 steps for a week when the old programming wins.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 11 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"You now have words ready for most hard moments.\", \"s\": [\"List the hard-moment phrases you wrote this week.\", \"Which have you already used for real?\", \"Put the best ones on your rescue card.\"], \"a\": \"Keep the rescue card in your wallet or as your phone wallpaper.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Maintenance Plan\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"After Day 90 there are no daily cards. A simple plan keeps the reprogramming going.\", \"s\": [\"Daily: your minimum routine (e.g. morning push, one card out loud, wind-down track).\", \"Weekly: one new phrase rewritten, and a check of your levels.\", \"Monthly: re-record your master script.\"], \"a\": \"Put the monthly re-record in your calendar as a repeating event.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Next Script Cycle\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"There's always another area to reprogram.\", \"s\": [\"Choose the next area for the coming month.\", \"Write 8 Level IV lines for it.\", \"Add them to your master script.\"], \"a\": \"Listen to the new lines tonight.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Letter in Self-Talk Words\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Writing to yourself 90 days from now, in Level IV language, sets the direction ahead.\", \"s\": [\"Write a one-page letter to yourself, dated 90 days from today.\", \"Describe your life then, in present tense.\", \"Seal it or save it, and set a reminder to read it.\"], \"a\": \"Read the letter's best line out loud now.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Wins List\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Seeing your progress makes the new self-talk believable.\", \"s\": [\"Flip through your notebook, cards and evidence \"Your One-Page Self-Talk Guide\", \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"A one-page guide written by you keeps the method close.\", \"s\": [\"Head a page \\\"My Self-Talk.\\\"\", \"Write: the Five Levels, my daily routine, my 10 best lines, my rescue card, my restart steps.\", \"Keep it at the front of your notebook.\"], \"a\": \"Photograph it and keep it on your phone.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Self-Talk color-scheme:dark}\n*{box-sizing:border-box}\n[hidden]{display:none!important}\nbody{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:16px/1.55 60px}\nheader.top{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:baseline;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px 16px;margin-bottom:4px}\n.sub{color:var(--muted);font-size:.9rem;margin:0 0 12px}\nh1{font:400 1.7rem/1.15 var(--f-display);margin:0;text-wrap:balance}\nh2{font:400 1.35rem/1.2 var(--f-display);margin:0 0 solid var(--line)}\nnav.tabs button{flex:0 0 auto;border:0;background:transparent;color:var(--muted);font:600 .92rem var(--f-body);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer}\nnav.tabs button[aria-selected=\"true\"]{background:var(--accent-soft);color:var(--accent)}\nbutton,select,textarea,input{font:inherit;color:inherit}\n:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r);padding:18px}\n.daybar{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}\n.daynum{font:500 .85rem 14px;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600}\n.btn.primary{background:var(--accent);border-color:var(--accent);color:var(--surface)}\n.btn:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default}\n.row{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center}\n.why{color:var(--muted);margin:4px 12px;font-style:italic;color:var(--muted)}\n.action{background:var(--accent-soft);border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;font-weight:500}\n.action b{color:var(--accent)}\n.scale{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(var(--n),minmax(0,1fr));gap:3px}\n.scale button{height:46px;border:1px solid transparent;border-radius:5px;cursor:pointer;font:500 .78rem var(--f-mono);padding:0;min-width:0}\n.scale button.g{background:var(--z-green-soft);color:var(--z-green)}\n.scale button.y{background:var(--z-yellow-soft);color:var(--z-yellow)}\n.scale button.r{background:var(--z-red-soft);color:var(--z-red)}\n.scale input{width:18px;height:18px;margin-top:2px;accent-color:var(--accent);flex:none}\n.when{font:500 .7rem span{display:block;height:100%;background:var(--accent);width:0}\n.weeks{display:grid;gap:14px}\n.daygrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(150px,1fr));gap:8px}\n.dcell{text-align:left;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface);border-radius:8px;padding:10px;cursor:pointer;display:grid;gap:2px;min-width:0}\n.dcell .n{font:500 .72rem var(--f-mono);color:var(--muted)}\n.dcell .n{color:var(--z-green)}\n.dcell.current{border-color:var(--accent);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--accent)}\n.tk{display:grid;gap:10px}\n.tool{border:1px solid summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}\n.tool .num{font:500 .8rem var(--f-mono);color:var(--saffron);min-width:28px}\n.tool .nm{font-weight:600;flex:1;min-width:0}\n.tool .rg{font:500 .72rem var(--f-mono);color:var(--muted);white-space:nowrap}\n.tool .body{padding:0 14px 14px 52px}\n.tool .body p{margin:0 0 10px;max-width:100%}\n.res-list{display:grid;gap:8px}\n.res-item{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:12px 14px;background:var(--surface)}\n.res-item b{display:block;margin-bottom:2px}\n.callout{border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--accent-soft);border-radius:8px;padding:12px 14px;font-size:.92rem}\n.stats{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:10px}\n.stat{background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:12px}\n.stat \"Your success depends on the steps you plan.\", \"steps\": [\"Write the Core Four: Life, Business, Time and Client plans.\", \"Work out your numbers: income → sales → conversion → daily attempts.\", \"Build it all in a focused 90-Day Burn.\"]}, {\"n\": 5, \"name\": \"The Law of Leverage\", \"meta\": \"Day 9\", \"use\": \"You're less likely to fail when you've told others you'll succeed.\", \"steps\": [\"Personal: self-discipline and written commitments.\", \"Associate: an accountability partner you report to weekly.\", \"Professional: a coach or mentor.\"]}, {\"n\": 6, \"name\": \"The [\"Describe your ideal client.\", \"Set daily prospecting numbers from your business plan.\", \"Use the approach steps: sources, research, letter, follow-up, appointment.\"]}, {\"n\": 10, \"name\": relevant impact.\", \"Offer solutions tied to their stated values and needs.\", \"Watch for positive buying signals, then ask for the business.\"]}, {\"n\": 13, \"name\": \"The Law of Incubation\", \"meta\": \"Days 23–25\", \"use\": \"The most profitable relationships grow over time.\", \"steps\": [\"Rank clients in 3 tiers: VIP, Premier, Standard.\", \"Decide how much time and money to reinvest How you see it decides your direction.\", \"s\": [\"List your last 5 lost sales or rejections.\", \"Write one lesson from each.\", \"Write: \\\"Each no moves me closer to the next yes.\\\"\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Make one call you've been avoiding.\"}, {\"t\": \"Think Like a CEO\", \"x\": 3, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Top salespeople run their careers like owners, investing in themselves.\", \"s\": [\"Compare your habits: busy or productive?
