Schedule priorities or prioritise your schedule?\", \"Mark 3 \\\"salesperson\\\" habits to change.\", \"Choose one investment in yourself: course, tool, book or coach.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Book or buy that investment this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Life Plan\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Your business should serve your life, not swallow it. Start the Core Four with your Life Plan.\", \"s\": [\"Write what you want in health, family, finances and personal growth this year.\", \"Note the time each needs every week.\", \"Mark non-negotiable family and health time.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Put one family or health block into your calendar.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Business Plan Numbers\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"The ladder: income goal → sales needed → conversion rate → daily attempts.\", \"s\": [\"Write your yearly income goal.\", \"Divide by average earnings per sale: sales needed.\", \"Use your conversion rate to find daily sales attempts.\"], \"e\": \"₹12 lakh/year ÷ ₹10,000 per sale = 120 sales. At 25% conversion → 480 attempts → about 2 a working day.\", \"a\": \"Hit today's attempt number.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 1 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Foundation check: purpose, plans and numbers.\", \"s\": [\"Review your time log: where did your hours really go?\", \"Check your Purpose scores.\", \"Write one change for next week.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Share your purpose statement with someone you trust.\"}, {\"t\": \"Time Plan & Client Plan\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Complete the Core Four with how you'll use your time and serve your clients.\", \"s\": [\"Time Plan: when you prospect, sell, serve and rest each week.\", \"Client Plan: who your best clients are and how you'll serve them.\", \"Put both on one page with your Life and Business Plans.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Follow your Time Plan for one full day.\"}, {\"t\": \"Get Accountable\", \"x\": 5, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"You're less likely to fail when you've told others you'll succeed.\", \"s\": [\"Choose an accountability partner (colleague, friend or mentor).\", \"Agree on one weekly number to report.\", \"Write your commitment and send it to them.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'll make 10 prospecting calls a day and report each Friday.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Send the message today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Hourly Rate & 80/20\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Knowing what an hour is worth shows you what not to do.\", \"s\": [\"Hourly rate = yearly income goal ÷ working hours a year.\", \"From your time log, find the 20% of activities that bring 80% of results.\", \"Mark tasks worth less than your hourly rate.\"], \"e\": \"₹12 lakh ÷ 2,000 hours = ₹600 an hour. Filing that takes an hour isn't worth ₹600 of your time.\", \"a\": \"Hand off one low-value task today.\"}, {\"t\": \"The 100 Percent Question\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"What if you spent all your time on the one or two things with the biggest impact?\", \"s\": [\"Name the 1–2 activities that bring you the most results and joy.\", \"List everything else.\", \"Mark each: keep, delegate, automate or drop.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Delegate or drop one item this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Red Light, Green Light\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Clean up your business before you build it up.\", \"s\": [\"List your red-light activities (paperwork, interruptions, low-value clients, gossip).\", \"List your green-light ones (prospecting, referrals, adding value to key clients).\", \"Estimate the % of your week on each.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Do your first 2 hours tomorrow on green-light work only.\"}, {\"t\": \"Block Your Ideal Week\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Time blocking means scheduling your priorities, not prioritising your schedule.\", \"s\": [\"Draw your week in blocks.\", \"Put green-light blocks first, in your best hours.\", \"Batch email, calls and admin into set blocks.\"], \"e\": \"Mon–Fri 9–11 am: prospecting. 11–1: appointments. 4–5 pm: email and admin.\", \"a\": \"Follow the blocks tomorrow.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 2 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Is your business cleaner?\", \"s\": [\"Compare your Green-light scores in Weeks 1 and 2.\", \"Report to your accountability partner.\", \"Adjust your time blocks.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Reward yourself for two weeks of focus.\"}, {\"t\": \"Dress Rehearsal\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Top performers rehearse. Sales is a performance in four acts.\", \"s\": [\"List the top 10 reasons clients choose you, and the top 10 for your company.\", \"Write a short script showing each one.\", \"Practise your 3 most important scripts out loud.\"], \"e\": \"You: experience, responsiveness, honesty. Company: delivery, range, after-sales service.\", \"a\": \"Rehearse with a colleague or in front of a mirror.\"}, {\"t\": \"Objections as Opportunities\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Objections are chances to build more trust, not obstacles.\", \"s\": [\"List your 5 most common objections.\", \"For each, write a response that listens first, then informs.\", \"Practise them aloud.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"It's too expensive\\\" → \\\"I understand. What's most important to you in this decision?\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use one response in a real conversation.\"}, {\"t\": \"Aim for the Bull's-Eye\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"If you don't aim for the best prospects, you'll do business with any prospect.\", \"s\": [\"Describe your ideal client: who, what they need, what they're worth.\", \"List where to find them.\", \"Set your daily prospecting number.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Contact 3 ideal prospects today.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Trust-Based Approach\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A planned approach makes prospects glad to meet you.\", \"s\": [\"Pick 5 prospects from your best sources and research them.\", \"Write a short, value-focused approach message.\", \"Plan your follow-up call and appointment request.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Send the 5 messages today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Fewer, Better Clients\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"For more business, have fewer clients and serve them better.\", \"s\": [\"List your clients with yearly value and effort (high/low).\", \"Mark high-value/low-effort ones as priorities.\", \"Choose 1–2 low-value/high-effort clients to refer or release.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Do something extra for one top client.\"}, {\"t\": \"The High Trust Interview\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"People buy feelings. Learn what matters to them before you present.\", \"s\": [\"Write a 45–60 second high-impact opening.\", \"Write 3 values questions (\\\"What's important to you about…?\\\").\", \"Write 3 questions about their highest-value needs (speed, quality, access…).\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Use the questions in your next meeting.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 3 Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Selling with trust: check your skills.\", \"s\": [\"Which script worked best this week?\", \"Which objection still trips you up?\", \"Report to your accountability partner.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Celebrate three weeks: do something you enjoy.\"}, {\"t\": \"Hook Them Early\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A captivated client stays to the end.\", \"s\": [\"Role-play your full interview with a friend.\", \"Match each solution to a value or need they mentioned.\", \"Note the buying signals you see.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Ask clearly for the business in your next suitable meeting.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rank Your Clients\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Your best relationships need the most care.\", \"s\": [\"Rank clients into VIP, Premier and Standard.\", \"Note the % of sales each tier brings.\", \"Choose your top 5 VIPs.\"], \"e\": \"5 VIP clients = 60% of sales; 60 Premier = 25%; 35 Standard = 15%.\", \"a\": \"Call one VIP just to add value.\"}, {\"t\": \"Invest and Stay in Touch\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Decide how much to reinvest in each tier, and how often you'll make contact.\", \"s\": [\"Set a reinvestment level per tier (e.g. 15%, 10%, 5% of client value).\", \"Plan yearly contacts per tier (e.g. VIP monthly, Premier quarterly).\", \"Put the next contacts in your calendar.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Send something useful to 3 clients today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Client Lifetime Value\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A loyal client is worth far more than one sale, including referrals.\", \"s\": [\"Average sale × sales per year = yearly value.\", \"× years they stay = client value.\", \"Add referral value. What's one client really worth?\"], \"e\": \"₹5,000 × 4 a year × 5 years = ₹1 lakh, plus referrals.\", \"a\": \"Ask one happy client for a referral.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your Service Standards\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Service is the umbrella over high trust selling.\", \"s\": [\"Decide how good your service will be.\", \"Write 5 core values and service standards.\", \"Share them with your team or clients.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"We return every call the same day.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Live one standard visibly today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Overdeliver & Recover\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Underpromise, overdeliver, and fix problems brilliantly.\", \"s\": [\"Pick one current order and plan one unexpected extra.\", \"Write your client-recovery steps for when things go wrong.\", \"Survey one client: how are we doing?\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Deliver the extra today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your 90-Day Burn\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Real change needs a focused build-out period.\", \"s\": [\"Choose the 3 biggest changes for your business.\", \"Plan them over 90 days, week by week.\", \"Share the plan with your accountability partner.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Start week 1 of the plan tomorrow.\"}, {\"t\": \"How Far Have I Come?\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Measure the month.\", \"s\": [\"Open the Progress tab and fill the Day 30 column.\", \"Look at your Green-light line over 30 days.\", \"Compare this month's numbers with Day 1.\"], \"e\": \"\", \"a\": \"Share your progress with your accountability partner.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 1 Review: Selling for Life\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Phase 1 is done. High trust selling is a lifelong way of working.\", \"s\": [\"Choose your 5 favourite tools from this workbook.\", \"Decide which daily habits you'll keep.\", \"Set your sales and service goals for Days 31–90.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate. You finished Phase 1! Tomorrow, Phase 2 begins.\"}, {\"t\": \"Burn Week 1 Plan\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A plan for the week turns the 90-Day Burn from an idea into calendar time.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your Day 28 burn plan and Day 6 numbers.\", \"Write this week's targets: attempts, appointments, sales.\", \"Book the green-light blocks you need to hit them.\"], \"a\": \"Send this week's targets to your accountability partner.\"}, {\"t\": \"Purpose as a Filter\", \"x\": 1, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Duncan describes purpose as a decision filter. Use it on real choices.\", \"s\": [\"Write your purpose statement on a card.\", \"List 3 decisions you face this week.\", \"For each, ask: \\\"Does this fit my purpose?\\\" and decide.\"], \"a\": \"Say no to one thing that doesn't fit.\"}, {\"t\": \"Track Your Daily Numbers\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"10 min + all day\", \"w\": \"Your business plan only works if you hit the daily numbers it needs.\", \"s\": [\"Make a simple daily log: attempts, conversations, appointments, sales.\", \"Fill it in as you work today.\", \"Compare with your Day 6 target.\"], \"a\": \"Keep the log every working day of the burn.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Weekly Report Format\", \"x\": 5, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Accountability works best when the report is short and the same every week.\", \"s\": [\"Choose 4 numbers to report (e.g. attempts, appointments, sales, green-light %).\", \"Add one win and one lesson.\", \"Agree a fixed day and time with your partner.\"], \"a\": \"Send your first report this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Time-Block Audit\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Blocks drift. A weekly check keeps green-light time protected.\", \"s\": [\"Compare last week's planned blocks with what really happened.\", \"Note what broke the blocks: calls, crises, other people.\", \"Fix the cause for next week.\"], \"a\": \"Turn off notifications during tomorrow's first block.\"}, {\"t\": \"Clear Three Red Lights\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Every red-light task you remove gives time back to selling and serving.\", \"s\": [\"List this week's red-light tasks.\", \"Choose 3 to delegate, automate or drop.\", \"Act on all 3 this week.\"], \"e\": \"Use templates for common emails; hand paperwork to an assistant; batch calls into one block.\", \"a\": \"Remove the first one today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 5 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"The first burn week shows what the plan really needs.\", \"s\": [\"Compare your numbers with this week's targets.\", \"Compare your green-light scores with Week 4.\", \"Send your weekly report.\"], \"a\": \"Adjust next week's targets if they were too high or too low.\"}, {\"t\": \"Twenty Prospect Sources\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A long list of good sources means you never run out of ideal prospects.\", \"s\": [\"List 20 places where your ideal clients can be found: referrals, associations, events, online groups.\", \"Mark the 5 best.\", \"Plan one action for each of the 5.\"], \"a\": \"Take one of those actions today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Research Ten Prospects\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Knowing a prospect before you approach shows respect and builds trust.\", \"s\": [\"Choose 10 ideal prospects.\", \"For each, note what they do, a likely need, and a connection you share.\", \"Rank them by fit.\"], \"a\": \"Approach the top 3 this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Approach Messages\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A short, personal message about their needs opens doors that cold pitches close.\", \"s\": [\"Write a value-focused approach message for each of your top 5.\", \"Mention something specific about them.\", \"Say when you'll follow up.\"], \"a\": \"Send all 5 today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Follow-Up and Appointment Script\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Most appointments come from the follow-up, not the first message.\", \"s\": [\"Write a short follow-up call script.\", \"Include one sentence about the value of meeting.\", \"Practise it out loud three times.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I sent you a note on Monday. I'd like 20 minutes to understand what matters most to you about ___. Would Thursday or Friday suit you?\\\"\", \"a\": \"Make your follow-up calls today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Ask for Referrals\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Referrals from happy clients are the warmest prospects you can get.\", \"s\": [\"List 5 happy clients.\", \"Write a simple, respectful referral request.\", \"Decide when you'll ask each one.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"I'm glad this worked well for you. Who else do you know who might value the same help?\\\"\", \"a\": \"Ask at least two clients this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"The Rejection Log\", \"x\": 2, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"More prospecting means more \\\"no\\\". Logging the lessons turns rejection into progress.\", \"s\": [\"List this week's rejections.\", \"Write one lesson from each.\", \"Count them: each one is a step up the summit.\"], \"a\": \"Make one more call than planned today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 6 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"See which prospecting actions are working.\", \"s\": [\"Which source produced the most appointments?\", \"Which message or script got the best response?\", \"Send your weekly report.\"], \"a\": \"Double down on your best source next week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Record Your Opening\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Hearing yourself shows what needs polishing before a client hears it.\", \"s\": [\"Record your 45–60 second high-impact opening on your phone.\", \"Listen back: is it clear, warm and about them?\", \"Rewrite and re-record once.\"], \"a\": \"Use the new opening in your next meeting.\"}, {\"t\": \"Values Questions in Real Life\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"Values questions only become natural with real use.\", \"s\": [\"Use at least one \\\"What's important to you about…?\\\" question in 3 conversations.\", \"Listen without interrupting.\", \"Write down the values you heard.\"], \"a\": \"Tonight, note how each conversation felt different.\"}, {\"t\": \"Map Their Highest-Value Needs\", \"x\": 11, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Clients choose based on the 3–5 needs that matter most to them.\", \"s\": [\"Pick a current prospect.\", \"Write their likely highest-value needs: quality, speed, access, price, service.\", \"Plan questions to confirm them.\"], \"a\": \"Ask the questions in your next conversation with them.\"}, {\"t\": \"Match Solutions to Values\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A solution tied to what the client said matters to them is far more convincing than a list of features.\", \"s\": [\"Take the values and needs from Days 46–47.\", \"For each, write how your product or service meets it.\", \"Practise presenting the top 3 in the client's own words.\"], \"a\": \"Use this in your next presentation.\"}, {\"t\": \"Objection Drills\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Rehearsed answers come out calm under pressure.\", \"s\": [\"Ask a colleague or friend to throw your 5 common objections at you.\", \"Answer each one: listen, ask, then inform.\", \"Improve the weakest answer.\"], \"a\": \"Use the improved answer this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Buying Signals and the Ask\", \"x\": 12, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Trust-based selling still asks for the business, at the right moment.\", \"s\": [\"List the positive buying signals you've seen: questions about delivery, price, next steps.\", \"Write 2 natural ways to ask for the business.\", \"Practise them out loud.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"It sounds like this fits what matters most to you. Shall we get started on the first order?\\\"\", \"a\": \"Ask clearly in your next suitable meeting.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 7 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check how your conversations have changed.\", \"s\": [\"How many conversations used values questions?\", \"How many asks did you make, and how many said yes?\", \"Send your weekly report.\"], \"a\": \"Thank a client who said yes this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Plan for Each VIP\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Your few VIP clients often bring most of your business. Each deserves a plan.\", \"s\": [\"For each VIP client, write their goals for this year.\", \"Write one way you can help them reach those goals.\", \"Schedule your next contact with each.\"], \"a\": \"Call one VIP today with something useful, not a pitch.\"}, {\"t\": \"Touch Your Premier Clients\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Premier clients can become VIPs with steady care.\", \"s\": [\"List your Premier clients.\", \"Choose a simple, useful touch for this month: an article, an update, a check-in call.\", \"Send it to at least 5.\"], \"a\": \"Note any replies and follow up within a day.\"}, {\"t\": \"Survey Your Clients\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Duncan recommends surveying clients before, during and after you serve them.\", \"s\": [\"Write 3 short questions: what matters most, how are we doing, what could be better.\", \"Send them to 5 clients at different stages.\", \"Plan when you'll read and act on the answers.\"], \"a\": \"Thank each client who replies.\"}, {\"t\": \"Map Your Impression Points\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Every contact a client has with you leaves an impression, good or bad.\", \"s\": [\"List every point of contact: first call, emails, meetings, delivery, invoices, follow-up.\", \"Rate each 1–10 from the client's side.\", \"Improve the lowest one.\"], \"a\": \"Make that improvement this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Client Recovery Drill\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"A problem handled brilliantly can build more trust than no problem at all.\", \"s\": [\"Remember a time something went wrong for a client.\", \"Write how you'd handle it now: listen, apologise, fix, add something extra, follow up.\", \"Keep these steps where you can find them fast.\"], \"a\": \"Check in with one client who had a recent problem.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rebalance the Scale\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Fewer, better clients free time for the ones who matter most.\", \"s\": [\"Review your client list from Day 19.\", \"Choose one low-value, high-effort client to refer elsewhere or serve more simply.\", \"Use the time saved for a VIP or a new ideal prospect.\"], \"a\": \"Have the conversation or make the change this week, politely.\"}, {\"t\": \"Invest in Your Growth\", \"x\": \"3, 5\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A CEO invests in the business. A coach is the highest level of accountability.\", \"s\": [\"List one skill that would most raise your sales.\", \"Find a course, book, mentor or coach for it.\", \"Set a budget and a start date.\"], \"a\": \"Book or buy it this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Numbers Check\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Halfway through the burn, check the plan against the results.\", \"s\": [\"Total your daily logs since Day 33.\", \"Compare attempts, appointments and sales with your plan.\", \"Adjust your daily numbers for Phase 3.\"], \"a\": \"Share the updated numbers with your accountability partner.\"}, {\"t\": \"Phase 2 Review\", \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"Two-thirds done. See how far you've come before the final phase.\", \"s\": [\"On the Progress tab, fill the Day 60 column.\", \"Compare Day 1, Day 30 and Day 60.\", \"Write the 3 biggest changes since Day 30, and choose one target for a 7-day growth sprint in Phase 3.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate 60 days with your accountability partner.\"}, {\"t\": \"Design Your Own Routine\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"A routine you designed yourself is the one you'll keep.\", \"s\": [\"Look at the daily checklist. Mark the habits that made the biggest difference.\", \"Write your own routine: morning, working day, evening.\", \"Keep it simple enough for a busy day.\"], \"e\": \"Morning: purpose card + today's numbers. Day: 2-hour prospecting block, one VIP touch. Evening: daily log + tomorrow's blocks.\", \"a\": \"Follow your routine from today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Coach Someone Else\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Each chapter of the book ends with an application for sales leaders. Teaching a law to someone else deepens your own mastery.\", \"s\": [\"Choose a newer colleague, team member or friend in sales.\", \"Teach them one law that helped you most, with your own example.\", \"Agree one action they'll try this week.\"], \"a\": \"Check in with them in 7 days.\"}, {\"t\": \"Your CEO Review\", \"x\": 3, \"m\": \"25 min\", \"w\": \"A CEO steps back regularly to review the whole business.\", \"s\": [\"Review: income, best clients, best sources, time use, service quality.\", \"Write 3 things to keep, 3 to stop and 3 to start.\", \"Decide your top priority for the next month.\"], \"a\": \"Put the top priority in your calendar as a weekly block.\"}, {\"t\": \"Recalculate Your Hourly Rate\", \"x\": 6, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"As your income grows, more tasks fall below your hourly rate.\", \"s\": [\"Recalculate your hourly rate with your updated numbers.\", \"Ask the 100 Percent Question again.\", \"Choose one more task to delegate or drop.\"], \"a\": \"Delegate or drop it this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Full Green-Light Day\", \"x\": 7, \"m\": \"all day\", \"w\": \"One day of only green-light work shows what's possible.\", \"s\": [\"Plan tomorrow with only green-light activities in your working hours.\", \"Move all admin to before or after.\", \"Tonight, compare results with a normal day.\"], \"a\": \"Schedule one full green-light day every week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Life Plan Check\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Your business serves your life. Make sure the burn isn't burning you out.\", \"s\": [\"Reread your Day 5 Life Plan.\", \"Rate this month for health, family and personal time, 1–10.\", \"Protect one area that has slipped.\"], \"a\": \"Book that time in your calendar this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Week 9 Review\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Check that your own system works.\", \"s\": [\"Count the days you followed your routine.\", \"Compare your green-light scores with Week 8.\", \"Send your weekly report.\"], \"a\": \"Do something relaxing just for you.\"}, {\"t\": \"Choose Your Sprint Target\", \"x\": 4, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"One focused week shows how much the system can produce.\", \"s\": [\"Choose one target you can move a lot in 7 days: appointments, referrals, a key account.\", \"Write it with a number and the end date.\", \"Tell your accountability partner.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"By next Monday I have booked 8 appointments with ideal prospects.\\\"\", \"a\": \"Block your sprint time for all 7 days now.\"}, {\"t\": \"Sprint Numbers\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Working back from the target tells you exactly what each day needs.\", \"s\": [\"Work back from your target to daily attempts using your conversion rates.\", \"Write each day's number on a card.\", \"Prepare your prospect list for the whole week.\"], \"a\": \"Hit today's number.\"}, {\"t\": \"Rehearse for the Sprint\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"More conversations this week means your scripts must be sharp.\", \"s\": [\"Rehearse your opening, values questions and top 3 objections.\", \"Record one run-through and listen back.\", \"Fix the weakest part.\"], \"a\": \"Use the improved version in today's calls.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Power Prospecting Block\", \"x\": 9, \"m\": \"2 hours\", \"w\": \"Two uninterrupted hours of prospecting often beats a scattered week.\", \"s\": [\"Block 2 hours with phone on silent except for calls.\", \"Work only through your prospect list.\", \"Log every attempt and result.\"], \"a\": \"Book your follow-ups before the block ends.\"}, {\"t\": \"VIP Referrals\", \"x\": 13, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"Your best clients know people like them.\", \"s\": [\"Choose 3 VIP clients who are happy with your work.\", \"Ask each for one introduction, respectfully.\", \"Thank them, whatever the answer.\"], \"a\": \"Contact every introduction within 24 hours.\"}, {\"t\": \"Mid-Sprint Check\", \"x\": 5, \"m\": \"10 min\", \"w\": \"A quick check halfway keeps the sprint on track.\", \"s\": [\"Compare your progress with the target.\", \"Send a short update to your accountability partner.\", \"Decide what to change for the last days.\"], \"a\": \"Make the change today.\"}, {\"t\": \"Sprint Review\", \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"See what one focused week produced.\", \"s\": [\"Did you hit the target? Score it 0–10.\", \"What helped most, and what got in the way?\", \"Write what you'll repeat in normal weeks.\"], \"a\": \"Celebrate the sprint, whatever the score.\"}, {\"t\": \"After a Run of Rejections\", \"x\": 2, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"A bad run tests your view of failure more than anything else.\", \"s\": [\"Write the rejections in your recent run and the lesson in each.\", \"Reread your purpose statement.\", \"Pick the easiest next call and make it.\"], \"a\": \"Make 3 calls today, however you feel.\"}, {\"t\": \"Losing an Important Client\", \"x\": 14, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"How you handle a loss shapes your reputation with everyone else.\", \"s\": [\"Write what happened, honestly, including your part.\", \"Plan a respectful conversation: listen, thank them, ask what you could do better.\", \"Write one change to your service so it doesn't happen again.\"], \"a\": \"Have the conversation, or send a gracious note, this week.\"}, {\"t\": \"Price Pressure\", \"x\": 8, \"m\": \"20 min\", \"w\": \"When price is the only topic, value hasn't been made clear yet.\", \"s\": [\"Write the price objections you hear most.\", \"For each, write a values question to ask first.\", \"Write how you'll connect your offer to what they said matters.\"], \"e\": \"\\\"Before we talk price, what would make this the right choice for you?\\\"\", \"a\": \"Use this approach in your next price conversation.\"}, {\"t\": \"A Difficult Client\", \"x\": 10, \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Some clients cost more in time and stress than they bring in.\", \"s\": [\"Name the client and list what they cost you: time, stress, discounts.\", \"Compare with what they bring in.\", \"Choose: set clear boundaries, serve more simply, or refer them on.\"], \"a\": \"Act on your choice this week, politely.\"}, {\"t\": \"When You're Overloaded\", \"x\": \"6, 7\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Overload usually means red-light work has crept back.\", \"s\": [\"List everything on your plate this week.\", \"Mark each green or red light.\", \"Delegate, delay or drop 3 red-light items.\"], \"a\": \"Protect tomorrow's first 2 hours for green-light work.\"}, {\"t\": \"Reputation Before Revenue\", \"x\": \"1, 3\", \"m\": \"15 min\", \"w\": \"Duncan contrasts salespeople who put revenue first with CEOs who put reputation first. Sign in to claude.ai and open this page again to save it to your account and resume on any device.\":\"Your progress is saved in this browser on this device. Open this page in the same browser to continue where you left off.\";el.append(t)}\n}\n(async function(){\n  let db=null,user=null;\n  try{if(window.claude&&window.claude.use){[db,user]=await Promise.all([window.claude.use(\"db\"),window.claude.use(\"user\")])}}catch(e){}\n  let id=null,nm=\"\";try{id=user?await user.id():null}catch(e){}\n  try{if(user&&id){const me=await user.me();nm=(me&&me.name)||\"\"}}catch(e){}\n  if(db&&id){\n    try{\n      const ref=db.doc(\"data/users/\"+id+\"/progress\");\n      const snap=await ref.get();store={kind:\"db\",ref};\n      if(snap.exists){S=normalize(snap.data());renderAll();if(!document.getElementById(\"p-progress\").hidden)renderProgress();setStatus(\"Saved to your account\")}\n      else if(local){setStatus(\"Saving…\");await ref.set(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S)));setStatus(\"Saved to your account\")}\n      else setStatus(\"Progress saves to your account\");\n      acct(true,nm);return;\n    }catch(e){}\n  }\n  acct(false,\"\");store={kind:\"local\"};setStatus(local?\"Saved on this device only\":\"Progress saves on this device\");\n})();\n})();\n<\/script>\n<\/body><\/html>"};
(function(){const ov=document.getElementById("wbov"),fr=document.getElementById("wbframe");
const name=id=>(BOOKS.find(b=>b.id===id)||{}).title||"";
function open(id){if(!WB[id])return;document.getElementById("wbtitle").textContent=name(id)+" · 90-day workbook";fr.srcdoc=WB[id];ov.hidden=false;document.body.style.overflow="hidden"}
function close(){ov.hidden=true;fr.srcdoc="";document.body.style.overflow=""}
document.addEventListener("click",e=>{const a=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('a[href^="#wb-"]');if(a){e.preventDefault();open(a.getAttribute("href").slice(4))}});
document.getElementById("wbclose").onclick=close;
const sec=document.createElement("section");sec.className="card";
const h=document.createElement("h3");h.textContent="90-day practice workbooks";h.style.marginBottom="8px";
const p=document.createElement("p");p.className="muted";p.style.margin="0 0 12px";p.textContent="Daily exercises for each book, with scores, a routine checklist and progress charts.";
const g=document.createElement("div");g.className="wbgrid";sec.append(h,p,g);document.querySelector(".wrap").append(sec);
BOOKS.forEach(b=>{const a=document.createElement("a");a.href="#wb-"+b.id;a.style.borderLeft="4px solid "+b.hue;a.textContent=b.title;const s=document.createElement("span");s.textContent=b.author+" · 90 days";a.append(s);g.append(a)});
})();
</script>
</body></html>
